# Data Model

Canonical catalog of data contracts that cross a boundary in this repo — the
cross-session memory files, the Claude Code Stop-hook memory-extraction
contracts, the PreToolUse general-purpose-ban hook I/O, the reviewer
verdict contract with its SubagentStop capture / Stop gate hooks, and the
installer's consumer-side manifest, opt-in pack lists, hook registration and opt-in doctrine block,
and the eval harness's isolation, release-arm sample, score, proxy-log and summary records.

**Last updated:** 2026-10-28

| 2026-10-28 | feat/eval-release-arms round 8 | For 0eee2c9, 538dbd9 (`git diff aaed954..HEAD -- eval/metrics`). Every `api-proxy.mjs`, `sample-row.mjs`, `summarize-samples.mjs`, `api-proxy-test.sh`, `run-release-arms-test.sh` and `eval/metrics/README.md` cite in the three `ReleaseArm*` sections below re-cut against the current files. `ReleaseArmProxyLogLine`: the 429 `cause:"cap"` is split. `cause:"inflight"` (`proxy concurrency: …`) is the in-flight limit: transient, not a denial, not a stop. `cause:"budget"` (`unit budget exhausted: …`) is the unit cap: not a denial, but a stop downstream. The summary gains `inflight_rejected` and `budget_rejected` and drops `cap_rejected`. The request-cap counter `count` (`PROXY_MAX_REQUESTS`) is now bumped only after admission, so 429s and earlier denials no longer use it up. A mid-pipe upstream response error now also destroys the client response. An SSE reply with no `message_delta` now parses to no usage, so a 2xx one is charged its reservation and counted in `unparsed`. Smoke item 1 now fails on model denials and `cause:"budget"`, and ignores `inflight` and `drain`; the round-7 "code disagreement" note is removed. Tests: `api-proxy-test.sh` sections 13-16, upstream modes `midfail` / `nodelta`, and an 8 s hard timeout in `abort-client.mjs`. `ReleaseArmSampleRow`: `proxy.inflight_rejected` and `proxy.budget_rejected` replace `cap_rejected`; new stop signal `unit budget exhausted`; UNVERIFIED that the CLI retries inflight 429s. `ReleaseArmSummary`: each group gains `proxy_inflight_429s`, `proxy_inflight_429s_per_sample` and `proxy_budget_429s`, replacing `proxy_cap_rejected`. `comparable` is now count-based, `{ok, rule, runs, required_complete, reasons, warnings}`; `MIN_RATE` and `RATE_TOLERANCE` are removed. The text output prints the rule, the counts and the `WARNING:` lines. |
| 2026-10-28 | feat/eval-release-arms round 7 | For 47de2d5, 09fac16 (`git diff 5f784b3..HEAD -- eval/metrics`). `ReleaseArmProxyLogLine` rewritten against the current `api-proxy.mjs` (all cites re-cut). The queue is gone: requests are admitted concurrently, up to `PROXY_MAX_INFLIGHT` (default 8, now passed by the harness), only if committed + every in-flight reservation + this one `<= PROXY_MAX_UNITS`. Otherwise an immediate 429 `proxy cap/concurrency: …` with `cause:"cap"`, counted in `cap_rejected`, not `denials`. Ids are assigned after admission. Fail closed on failures: once the body is written upstream, a client abort, network error or timeout is charged its reservation (notes `client aborted request` / `upstream request error` / `upstream response error`, status `0`; status `-1` gone). Before that, the charge is 0. New: `MAX_DEPTH` 32, validator errors returned as 400, a top-level field allowlist `BODY_KEYS` (replaces the `mcp_servers` / `container` / `service_tier` checks; `service_tier` is now rejected with any value), `source.type` only `base64`, and tool checks before messages. The startup line gains `max_inflight`, `start` lines gain `inflight`. The summary gains `cap_rejected` and `max_inflight_seen`, and drops `dropped` and `queue_wait_ms_*`. The `dropped` line, `queue_wait_ms` and the queue-full / queue-wait denials are removed, including the old abandoned-queue claim. Tests: `api-proxy-test.sh` rewritten (sections 1-12) and the new `fixtures/proxy/deep-client.mjs`. `ReleaseArmSampleRow`: new `settings` object (from `SMALL_MODEL` and `SET_*`, `run-release-arms.sh:694-695`; `BETAS_SHA256` at `run-release-arms.sh:197-198`). `proxy` gains `cap_rejected`, `max_inflight_seen` and, without a summary only, `reserved_unfinished_units`; it loses `dropped` and `queue_wait_ms`. The rebuild now counts unparsed by `units > 0` and aborts by note. New stop signals `no proxy summary` and `client aborted request`; cap 429s are not a stop. New `cost_source` `unknown: no proxy summary (…)` (cost `null`) for every no-summary row. `ReleaseArmSummary`: groups gain `proxy_cap_rejected`, `completion_rate`, `run_ids`, `sandboxes` and `settings`; `primary.proxy_queue_wait_ms_total` is removed. `comparable` is now `{ok, min_complete: 2, min_rate: 0.8, rate_tolerance: 0.2, reasons[]}`, and its `… differ` reasons give counts, not values. The text output prints `NO because:` lines. Citations into `run-release-arms.sh`, `run-release-arms-test.sh`, `README.md`, `sample-row.mjs` and `summarize-samples.mjs` were re-cut catalog-wide (change-log rows left as written). Code/doc disagreements found and fixed in the same round: the cost-gate comment and HARD CAP line no longer say "one request at a time" (`run-release-arms.sh:328-333`); smoke item 1 ignores `cause:"cap"` and `cause:"drain"` lines (`smoke-check.mjs:26`); README no longer says a cap 429 makes a sample incomplete (`README.md:249`); the text summary prints `completion` and `proxy_cap_429s` per arm (`summarize-samples.mjs:196`).  UNVERIFIED: that Claude Code retries cap 429s and needs no field outside `BODY_KEYS`; the `phase:"error"` line after an abort. Nothing was run. |
| 2026-10-28 | feat/eval-release-arms round 6 | For 4eb0ad7, e3f7bc5, bec7cd3 (`git diff dd361f8..HEAD -- eval/metrics`). `ReleaseArmProxyLogLine` rewritten against the current `api-proxy.mjs` (all line cites re-cut). New env knobs `PROXY_MAX_QUEUE` (8), `PROXY_MAX_QUEUE_WAIT_MS` (120000) and `PROXY_IMAGE_RESERVE` (1600). `PROXY_CACHE_READ_WEIGHT` must be `0 < w <= 1`; an invalid value, now including `0`, becomes 1. The harness now refuses such a value too (`run-release-arms.sh:147-149`). New fields: `queue_wait_ms` on `start` lines, an optional `note` on `end` lines (client closed before/after upstream responded; status `-1` / `0`), a new `{dropped, path, queue_wait_ms}` line, and `cause:"drain"` on shutdown denials, which no longer count in `denials`. The summary gains `drain_rejected`, `dropped`, `client_aborted`, `queue_wait_ms_total` and `queue_wait_ms_max`. The reservation adds images x `PROXY_IMAGE_RESERVE`. New validation: a content-block type allowlist (rejects `document`), `source.type` only `base64` / `text`, walked through messages, `system` and `tool_result.content`; `container`, a `service_tier` other than `auto` / `standard_only`, and an array `input_schema` are rejected. New rules for the queue bound, dropping and client-disconnect aborts. Tests: `api-proxy-test.sh` sections 9-13, `fixtures/proxy/abort-client.mjs`, and the `upstream.mjs` `stall` mode. `ReleaseArmSampleRow`: `proxy` gains `drain_rejected`, `dropped`, `client_aborted` and `queue_wait_ms{total,max}`. A drain denial is no longer in `denials` and adds the stop signal `proxy rejected N request(s) while shutting down (not model-caused)`. New `cost_source` values: `unknown: N cap units charged with no usage attributed` (cost `null`, never `$0`) and `proxy tokens x supplied prices (estimate; includes worst-case charges)`. `ReleaseArmSummary`: each group gains `cost_sources`, `price_missing`, `models`, `prompts`, `harness` and `primary.proxy_queue_wait_ms_total`. A new top-level `comparable` `{ok, min_complete: 2, reasons[]}` (exported `MIN_COMPLETE`), and the text output ends with an `ARMS COMPARABLE?` line. `run-release-arms.sh` (+3 lines after 146), `run-release-arms-test.sh` (+23 after 409), `eval/metrics/README.md`, `sample-row.mjs` and `summarize-samples.mjs` cites re-cut catalog-wide. Code gaps found (documented): `smoke-check.mjs` item 1 ignores `cause:"drain"` lines (fixed in round 6). A gone queued request whose wait timer fires first is counted and logged as `dropped`, not a denial (fixed in round 6). Drain-loop drops are not logged. A client abort after upstream responded also counts in `unparsed`. `comparable` ignores `harness_dirty` and `--small-model`. |
| 2026-10-28 | feat/eval-release-arms round 5 | For 9411dcb, e601224 (`git diff 2771b58..HEAD -- eval/metrics .github`). `ReleaseArmProxyLogLine` rewritten for the new `api-proxy.mjs`. New `phase` lines `start` / `end` / `error` / `unfinished`; `usage` is now `{input, cache_read, cache_creation, output}`, no longer summed. Start-failure rows, the two-failure abort with exit 1, and the `AOS_TEST_FAIL_START` hook are documented. New cost gate: units ceiling, and a dollar ceiling only when prices are supplied. `ReleaseArmSummary`: new `proxy_denials`; `primary` gains `proxy_cache_read_tokens`, `proxy_cache_creation_tokens` and `proxy_cap_units`; `cost_usd` renamed `cost_usd_estimate`; `incompleteReason` prefers the row's own reason. `ArmIsolationRecord`: config re-check now compares names and content, also runs before every later prompt, and runs under none; docker prompt env adds three model variables; every `run-release-arms.sh` citation re-cut. `ReleaseArmScore`: citations re-cut only (`score-sample.mjs` unchanged). Code/doc disagreements found and fixed in the same round: an invalid `PROXY_CACHE_READ_WEIGHT` now falls back to 1 (fail closed, `api-proxy.mjs:65`); an upstream network error or timeout is now charged the reservation (status 0, `api-proxy.mjs:180`); the README now says any proxy denial, lists `cache_control`, and states that the cost-gate bound holds only while upstream usage ≤ the reservation (`api-proxy-test.sh:56-60` allows cap + one request for a fake upstream that breaks that). Still true: the no-summary rebuild leaves worst-case bookings out of `tokens` but keeps them in `cap_units`. ---

### AgenticOsInstallManifest

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **Kind** | `persistence` |
| **Ingestion route** | File `.agentic-os-manifest.json` (`MANIFEST_NAME`, `install.sh:69`; `$ManifestName`, `install.ps1:101`) at each install root on the consumer machine: Claude dest `${CLAUDE_DIR:-$HOME/.claude}` (`install.sh:161`) or `install.ps1 -Dest` (default `$env:USERPROFILE\.claude`, `install.ps1:53`; resolved to `$Root`, `install.ps1:190`); with `install.sh --codex`, **two** manifests — the Codex skills dir (`${CODEX_SKILLS_DIR:-$HOME/.agents/skills}`, or `<project>/.agents/skills` with `--project`) and the Codex config dir (`${CODEX_HOME:-$HOME/.codex}`, or `<project>/.codex`) (`install.sh:167-181`, `install.sh:1753-1756`). **Written** by `write_manifest` (`install.sh:443-474`) from `sync_finish` (`install.sh:1214-1216`) and by `Write-Manifest` (`install.ps1:321-341`, called as `if (-not $DryRun) { Write-Manifest $Root $manifest }` at `install.ps1:1191`), only on a non-dry-run install that passed the hook check below. `install.sh` writes a fresh `mktemp "$root/.agentic-os-manifest.XXXXXX"` file (O_EXCL, beside the target), `chmod 644`, then `mv -f` over the manifest (`install.sh:451-471`). The temp path is held in the global `INFLIGHT` from `mktemp` (`install.sh:451`) until it is cleared right after the `mv` (`install.sh:472`); there is no local temp variable any more, so `cleanup` removes a half-written `.agentic-os-manifest.XXXXXX` when an error or signal stops the run before the `mv` (see `ConsumerDoctrineBlock` "Crash rollback"); `install.ps1` uses `Write-Beside` (`[System.IO.FileMode]::CreateNew` temp `.agentic-os.<random>` beside the target, UTF-8 without BOM, then `Move-Item -Force`, `install.ps1:229-237`, call `install.ps1:340`). **Doctrine rollback disarm:** after the `mv`, when the root is `DOC_MF_ROOT`, `write_manifest` sets `DOC_ROLLBACK=""` (`install.sh:473`), so the doctrine crash rollback no longer fires once that manifest is on disk; ps1 instead sets `$manifestLanded = $true` right after the `Write-Manifest` line, inside a `try` that starts at `Install-DoctrineBlock`, and its `finally` restores the doctrine target only while `$manifestLanded` is still `$false` (`install.ps1:1178-1212`). See `ConsumerDoctrineBlock` "Crash rollback". **A symlinked manifest is refused**: `install.sh` exits 1 up front in `sync_place` before reading it (`install.sh:1039-1042`) and again in `write_manifest` (`install.sh:445-448`); `install.ps1` throws up front in `Read-Manifest` (reparse point, `install.ps1:304`), which both install and uninstall call before acting, and again in `Write-Manifest` (`install.ps1:339`). `install.sh` uninstall does not refuse one; it reads entries through it (each still gated by `safe_rel`) and `rm -f` removes the link, not its target. **Read** at the start of every install (`install.sh:1043`, `install.ps1:1068`) and uninstall (`install.sh:1258` and `manifest_hook_names` `install.sh:1240-1242`, call `install.sh:1670-1671`; `install.ps1:796`, `install.ps1:805`) — possibly by a different release or by the other installer (`install.ps1:320`). On install, `sync_place` re-validates each line of the transient `$WORK/pack-adopt.<id>` work file (below) and merges only the well-formed ones into the entries it read (`install.sh:1044-1058`); ps1 likewise adds each `$script:PackAdopt` entry whose key passes the `Test-SafeRel` charset and whose value is 64 lowercase hex, and that is not already in `$old`, right after `$old = Read-Manifest $Root`, and `Warn`s `skip a malformed pack adoption entry …` for an entry failing either the key or the value check; a well-formed entry already in `$old` is skipped silently (`install.ps1:1068-1071`). The `doctrine` record (below) is read separately, from the doctrine root's manifest only, before install or uninstall starts: `read_doctrine_record` (`install.sh:688-692`, called `install.sh:1643-1644`) / `Read-DoctrineRecord` (`install.ps1:584-593`, called `install.ps1:781`). The `packs` record (below) is read on install, update and dry run only (not uninstall), before anything is staged, from **every** manifest root of the run, **except in `install.sh --codex --project`**, where neither the record nor the tracked files are read: `pack_plan` (`install.sh:1475-1536`, called `install.sh:1692` with `claude:$DEST` and `install.sh:1738` with both Codex roots) sets `proj=true` for `--codex --project` (`install.sh:1483`); otherwise it runs `read_packs_record` (`install.sh:1431-1439`) on each root's manifest (`install.sh:1484-1491`) and, when no root has the key, `pack_tracked` (`install.sh:1350-1363`) on each root that has a manifest, which reads the manifest's `files` through `read_manifest` (`install.sh:1354`); ps1 `Set-PackPlan` (`install.ps1:961-1001`, called `install.ps1:1029`) runs `Read-PacksRecord` (`install.ps1:868-877`) and, when that is `$null`, `Get-PackEvidence` (`install.ps1:886-901`, which calls `Read-Manifest`, `install.ps1:890`). **Deleted** by `--uninstall` / `-Uninstall` after unmodified tracked files are removed (`install.sh:1294-1295`, `install.ps1:825-826`). |
| **Source** | `install.sh` (lines 23-38, 69-86, 108-158, 160-215, 224-274, 335-393, 395-1297, 1299-1548, 1575-1582, 1617-1791); `install.ps1` (lines 14-35, 48-84, 101-104, 184-396, 551-834, 836-1001, 1029-1230); `assets/packs/games.txt`, `assets/packs/marketing.txt` (see `InstallPackList`) |

**Up-front refusals** (all before any file in the install root changes):

- **Claude DEST charset (every mode)** — `install.sh` rejects a Claude `DEST` (`${CLAUDE_DIR:-$HOME/.claude}`) outside `^[/.a-zA-Z0-9._\ -]+$` (shell metacharacters; a space passes this regex): `Error: unsafe CLAUDE_DIR/DEST (shell metacharacters not allowed): <DEST>`, exit 1 (`install.sh:161-165`). This applies to install, dry run and uninstall.
- **Flag combinations** — `--uninstall` with `--force` or `--prune-legacy` (`install.sh:141-144`; `install.ps1:61-63`) and `--uninstall --with-doctrine` (`Error: --with-doctrine does not apply to --uninstall (uninstall always removes the doctrine block).`, `install.sh:145-148`; ps1 `-Uninstall -WithDoctrine`, `install.ps1:64-66`) are rejected before anything else runs. Pack flags: `install.sh` accepts `--with-<pack>` / `--without-<pack>` only for a `<pack>` matching `^[a-z0-9-]+$` that is in `PACKS` (`PACKS="games marketing"`, `install.sh:79`), else `Error: unknown option: <arg> (packs: games marketing)`, exit 1 (`install.sh:115-122`); ps1 has the fixed switches `-WithMarketing`, `-WithGames`, `-WithoutMarketing`, `-WithoutGames` (`install.ps1:49-52`, mapped onto `$Packs = @('games', 'marketing')` at `install.ps1:70-76`). Any pack flag with `--uninstall` / `-Uninstall` gives `Error: --with-<pack> and --without-<pack> do not apply to --uninstall (uninstall removes every AgenticOS file, pack files included).` (`install.sh:149-152`; ps1 `-With<Pack>` / `-Without<Pack>` wording, `install.ps1:77-79`), and `--with-<p>` together with `--without-<p>` gives `Error: --with-<p> and --without-<p> contradict each other; give one of them.` (`install.sh:153-158`; `install.ps1:80-84`). Both exit 1 before anything else runs.
- **Pack lists** — on install, update and dry run, `pack_plan` / `Set-PackPlan` reads every `assets/packs/<pack>.txt` (`InstallPackList`) before anything is staged. A missing or symlinked list gives `Error: <f> is missing (or a symlink): this payload predates opt-in packs or is incomplete. … Nothing was changed.` and an entry that is not `skills/<name>` or `agents/<name>.md` gives `Error: <f> lists '<line>', which is not skills/<name> or agents/<name>.md. Nothing was changed.`; both exit 1 (`load_pack`, `install.sh:1320-1336`; `Read-PackList` throws, `install.ps1:853-866`). This guards a new installer run over a payload from before packs.
- **Doctrine source** — when `doctrine_plan` / `Set-DoctrinePlan` considers the doctrine (`--with-doctrine`, or a manifest record; see `ConsumerDoctrineBlock`) and the run is not uninstall, `$REPO_ROOT/assets/consumer/doctrine.md` must exist, else `Error: <path> is missing from this release — cannot install the doctrine. Nothing was changed.`, exit 1 (`install.sh:746-749`, path `install.sh:1634`; `install.ps1:665-667`, path `install.ps1:568`; ps1 requires a leaf). This also fires on a flagless run whose record is stale (block already removed), because the check precedes the target scan.
- **Claude hook-path gate (install, update and dry run only)** — right after the hook-library API gate (next-but-one bullet), and only when the platform is Claude and the run is not `--uninstall` (`install.sh:364`), `install.sh` walks up from `DEST` to its nearest existing ancestor and collects the not-yet-existing tail (`install.sh:365-369`). It then runs the lib's `_validate_hook_dir` on the given `DEST` (`install.sh:370`) and on the **canonical** DEST, i.e. `pwd -P` of that ancestor plus the tail (`install.sh:371`). `_validate_hook_dir` (`install-hook-settings.sh:19-31`) refuses any path not matching `^[/.a-zA-Z0-9._-]+$`, so whitespace and shell metacharacters are both refused (`install-hook-settings.sh:26`). It prints `Error: the Claude install dir resolves to a path with whitespace or shell metacharacters: <path>` / `  It would be pasted into hook commands. Install to a plain path. To move an existing install: run with --uninstall and the same CLAUDE_DIR, then reinstall elsewhere. Nothing was changed.` and exits 1 (`install-hook-settings.sh:27-29`). Reason (`install.sh:358-363`; `install-hook-settings.sh:19-24`): Claude hook commands are registered unquoted and use the canonical dir (`bash <DEST>/hooks/x.sh`; see `ConsumerHookRegistration`). `--uninstall` is exempt, so an install made into such a path before this check can still be removed: uninstall runs no hook command, only string-compares registered commands and deletes quoted manifest paths. The lib applies the same gate to the given and canonical DEST in `merge_claude_hook_settings` (`install-hook-settings.sh:272`, `install-hook-settings.sh:275`). `unregister_claude_hook_settings` does not call it (`install-hook-settings.sh:324-327`). The former top-of-file whitespace block in `install.sh` and the lib's `_reject_whitespace_dest` are gone. Codex paths may contain spaces (`^[/.a-zA-Z0-9._\ -]+$`, `install.sh:183-188`), because the Codex template quotes its commands. ps1 `-Dest` is limited to `^[/\\:.a-zA-Z0-9._-]+$` (no space, `install.ps1:58-60`). The resolved `$Root` is absolute (a relative `-Dest` resolves against the current dir, `GetUnresolvedProviderPathFromPSPath`, `install.ps1:190`) but **not** symlink-resolved. Unless `-Uninstall`, `$Root` must also match `^[/\\:.a-zA-Z0-9._-]+$`, else ps1 throws `Error: -Dest resolves to a path with whitespace or shell metacharacters (<Root>); it would be pasted into hook commands. Use a plain path (to move an existing install: -Uninstall with the same -Dest, then reinstall).` (`install.ps1:191-193`, skipped via `-not $Uninstall`). So, as in `install.sh`, `-Uninstall` is exempt.
- **Codex project scope** — with `--codex --project`, the project root must exist (`install.sh:195-198`). Each of `<project>/.codex`, `<project>/.agents` and `<project>/.agents/skills` must not be a symlink (`Error: <p> is a symlink. In project scope AgenticOS only writes into real directories inside the project; replace it with a directory, then re-run. Nothing was changed.`, `install.sh:202-205`). When it exists, its `pwd -P` must lie strictly under the project root's `pwd -P` (`Error: <p> resolves outside the project (<canon>). Nothing was changed.`, `install.sh:206-211`). Each failure exits 1 with one error, before any write (`install.sh:194-214`). Applies to install, dry run and uninstall. User-scope roots (`CODEX_HOME`, `CODEX_SKILLS_DIR`) are not checked and may be symlinks, by design.
- **Hook-library API** — `install.sh` first runs `unset AGENTIC_OS_HOOK_LIB_API` (`install.sh:335-336`) so the caller's environment cannot satisfy the gate, then `source`s `scripts/lib/install-hook-settings.sh` (`install.sh:338`). It requires `AGENTIC_OS_HOOK_LIB_API` (set to `2` in the lib, `install-hook-settings.sh:5-9`) to be an integer ≥ `REQUIRED_LIB_API=2` **and** `declare -F` to find `merge_claude_hook_settings`, `unregister_claude_hook_settings`, `merge_codex_hook_settings` and `unregister_codex_hook_settings`; otherwise `lib_api` is forced to `0` and it prints `Error: <REPO_ROOT>/scripts/lib/install-hook-settings.sh is older than this installer` / `(hook-library API <value or none>, need 2).` / `… Nothing was changed.` and exits 1 (`install.sh:345-356`). This guards a new `install.sh` run over an older release payload. Applies to install, dry run and uninstall. `install.ps1` does not source the lib (no equivalent check).
- **Linked shipped dirs** — `refuse_linked_dirs <root> <dirs…>` (`install.sh:575-585`): if `<root>/<d>` is a symlink for any listed dir, prints one `Error: <root>/<d> is a symlink. AgenticOS never writes or deletes through a symlink, so it cannot manage that directory.` plus `Replace it with a real directory … Nothing was changed.` and exits 1. Dirs: Claude `skills agents commands hooks` (`install.sh:1667` uninstall, `install.sh:1714` install), plus `agentic-os` on install and dry run **only when the doctrine file is staged** (`DOCTRINE=true`, i.e. doctrine mode `write` or `handsoff`, `install.sh:1658-1659`, check `install.sh:1715`; not checked on uninstall); Codex config dir `agents hooks` (`install.sh:1676` uninstall, `install.sh:1751` install). The Codex skills root is not checked here (in project scope it is covered by the bullet above). ps1 `Assert-NoLinkedDirs` (`install.ps1:278-285`) throws the same text for any reparse point among its `-Dirs` under `$Root`; the default is the literal list `skills agents commands hooks` (`install.ps1:278`), not `$ShippedPrefixes` (uninstall `install.ps1:794`, install `install.ps1:1064`); install also calls it with `agentic-os` when `$Doctrine` is set (`install.ps1:784`, `install.ps1:1065`). Applies to install, dry run and uninstall.
- **Doctrine target** — `doctrine_plan` / `Set-DoctrinePlan` (`install.sh:741-825`, called `install.sh:1656`; `install.ps1:662-705`, called `install.ps1:782`) decides the doctrine mode before any write, dry run and uninstall included. Only an explicit `--with-doctrine` / `-WithDoctrine` refuses here: a symlinked, non-regular or hard-linked target, malformed markers, a foreign block with no install record (`install.sh:786-789`, `install.ps1:691`), or a shipped block with no install record in user scope (`install.sh:798-801`, `install.ps1:692`) gives one error and exit 1 with nothing changed. In `install.sh --codex --project` the record is always treated as absent, and a shipped block is not refused: the current block gives mode `current`, an older shipped block mode `write` with `DOC_UPGRADE=true` (`install.sh:791-797`). A flagless run or an uninstall instead goes hands-off (warn, leave the target alone, carry on). See `ConsumerDoctrineBlock`.

**Shape** (exact byte layout of both writers — `install.sh:453-468`, `install.ps1:323-337`; the `packs` line is in every manifest these writers produce; the `doctrine` line appears only in the doctrine root's manifest while the doctrine is managed or hands-off, and never from `install.sh --codex --project`):

```json
{
  "schema": 1,
  "name": "agentic-os",
  "version": "v3.0.1",
  "source": "release",
  "platform": "claude",
  "packs": ["games", "marketing"],
  "doctrine": {"created": false, "block_sha256": "<64 lowercase hex>"},
  "files": [
    {"path": "agentic-os/doctrine.md", "sha256": "<64 lowercase hex>"},
    {"path": "agents/code-reviewer.md", "sha256": "<64 lowercase hex>"},
    {"path": "hooks/block-bad-bash.sh", "sha256": "<64 lowercase hex>"}
  ]
}
```

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `schema` | integer | yes | Literal `1` (`install.sh:453`, `install.ps1:323`). Not checked on read |
| `name` | string | yes | Literal `"agentic-os"` (`install.sh:453`, `install.ps1:323`). Not checked on read |
| `version` | string | yes | `VERSION` (`install.sh:60`) / `$Version` (`install.ps1:92`), e.g. `v3.0.1`; written even for a local-clone install. Not checked on read |
| `source` | enum string | yes | `"release"` (pinned tarball) \| `"local-clone"` (`SOURCE_KIND`, `install.sh:278`, `install.sh:281`; `$SourceKind`, `install.ps1:117`, `install.ps1:120`). Not checked on read |
| `platform` | enum string | yes | `"claude"` \| `"codex"` (`install.sh:454`) — `install.sh` passes `claude` (`install.sh:1717`) or `codex` for both Codex roots (`install.sh:1753-1754`); `install.ps1` always writes `"claude"` (`install.ps1:324`). Not checked on read |
| `packs` | array of string (pack names) | no on read; always written by this release | The opt-in packs this run installed. Written on its own line right after `platform` and before the optional `doctrine` line, as exactly `  "packs": [<names>],`, each name double-quoted and joined by `, `, so `  "packs": [],` means core only (`install.sh:455-458`; ps1 `install.ps1:325-327`). Names come from `PACKS_ON` / `$script:PacksOn`, which `pack_plan` / `Set-PackPlan` fill in `PACKS` / `$Packs` order (`games`, then `marketing`; `install.sh:79`, `install.sh:1495`, `install.sh:1534`; `install.ps1:70`, `install.ps1:965`, `install.ps1:999`), and each is the basename of an `assets/packs/<pack>.txt` (`InstallPackList`). Written by every non-dry-run install or update to **every** manifest root of the run, with the same value: Claude `DEST`, and for Codex both the skills root and the config root (`PACKS_ON` is one global; `write_manifest` is called per root from `sync_finish`, `install.sh:1214-1216`, calls `install.sh:1730`, `install.sh:1769-1770`). In `install.sh --codex --project` it is still written, though never read there (see below; comment `install.sh:1478-1482`). Never written by a dry run, and never by uninstall (which deletes the manifest). **Absent key** = a manifest written before packs existed (or by another writer); it is not an error. **Read** before anything is staged, on install, update and dry run (never on uninstall), and never in `install.sh --codex --project`, by `read_packs_record` (`install.sh:1431-1439`): returns failure when the manifest is missing or a symlink (`install.sh:1435`) or has no line matching `^  "packs": \[[^]]*\],$` (first match, `grep -m 1`, `install.sh:1436`), so another spacing, a missing trailing comma or a multi-line array reads as **no key**; otherwise it prints each `"[a-z0-9-]+"` token after the `[` (`install.sh:1437-1438`), so `[]` reads as key present, no packs. ps1 `Read-PacksRecord` (`install.ps1:868-877`) returns `$null` (no key) for a reparse-point or non-file manifest or when `ConvertFrom-Json` finds no `packs` property (`install.ps1:872-875`; malformed JSON throws), else the array's string values matching `^[a-z0-9-]+$` (`install.ps1:876`). Recorded names that are not in `PACKS` / `$Packs` are ignored. **Decision** per pack (`pack_plan`, `install.sh:1475-1536`; `Set-PackPlan`, `install.ps1:961-1001`), with reason `flag`, `recorded`, `tracked` or `ondisk` (empty = not installed): `--without-<pack>` / `-Without<Pack>` → not installed (overrides everything, `install.sh:1498`, `install.ps1:969`); else `--with-<pack>` → installed, `flag` (`install.sh:1500-1501`, `install.ps1:970`); else listed in the record of **any** manifest of this run's roots (`install.sh:1484-1491`, `install.sh:1502-1503`; ps1 has one root, `install.ps1:971`) → installed, `recorded`; else, only when **no** root's manifest has the key (`has_key=false`, `install.sh:1504`; `$null -eq $rec`, `install.ps1:972`), each root is tried in order until one gives evidence (`install.sh:1505-1513`): a root whose manifest exists (a regular file or a symlink) is judged **only** by `pack_tracked` (reason `tracked`, `install.sh:1507-1509`), a root with no manifest **only** by `pack_shipped_file` (reason `ondisk`, `install.sh:1510-1511`); ps1 `Get-PackEvidence` returns `'tracked'`, `"disk:<rel>"` or `''` (`install.ps1:973-974`); see "Pack evidence" below. Otherwise not installed. **Project scope (`install.sh --codex --project`):** `proj=true` (`install.sh:1483`), so no record is read (`has_key` stays `false`, `recorded` empty) and `pack_tracked` is never called: a pack is installed only by `--with-<pack>` or by `pack_shipped_file` evidence on either root, even when the manifests exist (`install.sh:1507`), because `.codex/` and `.agents/` are repository content (comment `install.sh:1478-1482`; the doctrine record is distrusted the same way, `DOC_PROJECT`). ps1 is Claude-only and has no project scope. So outside project scope, once a run has written the key, a pack absent from it stays uninstalled until `--with-<pack>`, even if its files are on disk. Each decision prints a `note` (`install.sh:1515-1533`; ps1 `install.ps1:976-998`): `<p>: installed (--with-<p>)`; `<p>: kept, as recorded in the install manifest; re-run with --without-<p> to remove it`; `<p>: kept, because the install manifest (written before packs existed) tracks its files; re-run with --without-<p> to remove it`; for `ondisk`, `<p>: kept, because AgenticOS copies of its files are installed here with no manifest (e.g. <rel>); re-run with --without-<p> to remove it`, or in project scope `<p>: kept, because AgenticOS copies of its files are installed in this project (e.g. <rel>; the project's manifest is repository content and is not trusted); re-run with --without-<p> to remove it`, where `<rel>` is the root-relative evidence file printed by `pack_shipped_file` (ps1: the `disk:` suffix, `install.ps1:974`, `install.ps1:980`); `<p>: not installed (--without-<p>); unmodified files from it are removed, edited ones kept`; `<p>: not installed (add it with --with-<p>)` (ps1 uses `-With<Pack>` / `-Without<Pack>`). A not-installed pack's entries are collected in `$WORK/packs.off` / `$script:PacksOff` (`install.sh:1526`, `install.ps1:982`) and removed from the stage (`unstage_packs`, `install.sh:1538-1548`, called `install.sh:1698` and `install.sh:1745`; Codex agents skipped in `install_codex_agents`, `install.sh:1582`; ps1 `install.ps1:1035-1038`), so their tracked files become "in manifest, not in release" in the lifecycle table below: unmodified ones are removed, edited ones kept and still tracked. **`--without-<pack>` / `-Without<Pack>` on a root with no manifest** (both installers): right after the `--without` note, `pack_plan` calls `pack_adopt` (`install.sh:1529`) and `Set-PackPlan` fills `$script:PackAdopt` (`install.ps1:985-994`); see "Pre-manifest `--without`" below. So the pack's AgenticOS copies on that root are pruned too; any other regular file in the pack's dirs there is left in place and named by a `keep … left in place, remove it by hand …` warning, a symlink on the way to a pack path or inside a pack dir is never followed or deleted and is named by a `skip … symlink, left in place …` warning, and a name outside the path charset is skipped and named (escaped). Otherwise a pack file no manifest tracks is never touched; in particular a pack that is merely not selected (no `--without` / `-Without<Pack>`) leaves untracked files alone |
| `doctrine` | object | no | The `ConsumerDoctrineBlock` record, written on its own line right after `packs` as exactly `  "doctrine": {"created": <true\|false>, "block_sha256": "<hex>"},` (`install.sh:460-462`, `install.ps1:328-330`). `install.sh` writes it only when `DOC_CREATED` is non-empty **and** the root is `DOC_MF_ROOT` **and** `DOC_PROJECT` is not `true` (`[[ -n "$DOC_CREATED" && "$root" == "$DOC_MF_ROOT" && "$DOC_PROJECT" != true ]]`, `install.sh:460`; comment `install.sh:459`): Claude `DEST` (`install.sh:1637`) and Codex user scope `CODEX_CONFIG_DIR` (`install.sh:1641`). In `--codex --project` (`DOC_MF_ROOT` = `<project>/.codex`, `install.sh:1639`) no record is ever written, even after a `write`-mode install. The Codex skills-root manifest never carries it. `DOC_CREATED` is set by `doctrine_apply` in mode `write` (`install.sh:892`, `install.sh:901-902`) and in mode `handsoff`, where the old record is carried forward (`install.sh:880`). In `--codex --project` an install never reaches `handsoff`: a flagless run is `off` and `--with-doctrine` either errors or gives `write` (first install, or upgrade of an older shipped block) / `current` (see `ConsumerDoctrineBlock`); a record already in that manifest is ignored on read (`install.sh:1647-1655`) and dropped by the rewrite. In modes `optout` (`install.sh:885-887`) and `current` (`install.sh:882-884`, project scope only) it stays empty, so the new manifest has **no** record. `install.ps1` has one root and writes it when `$script:DocCreated` is set (`install.ps1:328`; set in `Install-DoctrineBlock` `install.ps1:710` handsoff, `install.ps1:720`, `install.ps1:729` write; not in optout `install.ps1:714-717`). Manifests are written only on a non-dry run (`install.sh:1214-1216`, `install.ps1:1191`). With the doctrine off (no flag, no record) the line is absent. Sticky: a record present on read makes a flagless run consider the doctrine (`install.sh:745`, `install.sh:1643-1644`; `install.ps1:664`, `install.ps1:781`), but it is kept current only while the target still holds a shipped block (mode table in `ConsumerDoctrineBlock`). A record is **not** proof that a block is AgenticOS's (in project scope the repository ships the manifest, so there `install.sh` ignores the record entirely, see the read rules below). It decides only markers-with-flag (a well-formed block, shipped or foreign: `write` with a record, refreshing a foreign block after a `.bak`; hard error without one in user scope, `install.sh:786-801`, `install.ps1:691-692`), unsafe-on-uninstall (`handsoff` vs `off`) and shipped-block-on-uninstall (`write` vs `handsoff`, `install.sh:813-815`, `install.ps1:698-699`) |
| `doctrine.created` | boolean (JSON literal) | yes (in `doctrine`) | Set at each `write`-mode install: `true` only when the target did not exist and this installer created it (`install.sh:891-892`, `install.ps1:719-720`). For an existing file: `false` when it held no block (the block is added to the user's own file); when it already held a block, `true` only if the old record said `true`, else `false` (`DOC_CREATED=false`, then `true` when `DOC_B != 0` and `DOC_OLD_CREATED == true`, `install.sh:899-902`; `install.ps1:727-729`, `$d.B -ge 0 -and $Record -and $Record.created`). Hands-off carries the old value (`install.sh:880`, `install.ps1:710`). **Project scope (`install.sh --codex --project`):** the whole record is ignored on read and none is written. Right after it is read, `DOC_PROJECT=true` sets `DOC_OLD_SHA=""` and `DOC_OLD_CREATED=false` (`install.sh:1647-1655`). `doctrine_apply` still sets `DOC_CREATED` there (`true` when it creates `AGENTS.md`, `install.sh:892`; `false` when it upgrades an older shipped block in an existing file, `install.sh:901-902`), but `write_manifest` skips the `doctrine` line when `DOC_PROJECT` is `true` (`install.sh:460`). The former project-scope `DOC_CREATED=false` override in the create branch is gone. On uninstall (mode `write` only, which needs a record, so never in project scope): when `true`, the blank separator is **not** stripped and the target is deleted if empty after the block is removed; when `false`, one leading separator line is stripped when the block is at line 1 and the file is never deleted (`install.sh:993-1006`, `install.ps1:762-774`) |
| `doctrine.block_sha256` | string (64 hex, lowercase) | yes (in `doctrine`) | SHA-256 of the **LF-normalised** block: begin marker line, body, end marker line, each `\n`-terminated, whatever line ending was actually written (`doctrine_block` `install.sh:718-723` into `$WORK/doctrine.lf`, hashed `install.sh:750-751`; ps1 `$DocBlockLf` `install.ps1:571` hashed via `Get-TextSha` over Latin-1 bytes `install.ps1:579-582`, `install.ps1:668`). Hands-off keeps the old value (`install.sh:880`, `install.ps1:711`). The recorded value is not compared with the target any more: whether the current block is one AgenticOS shipped is decided from its LF-normalised sha (`doc_cur_sha` / `Get-CurBlockSha`) against the release block and, for Codex, `ConsumerDoctrineBlockShas` (`doc_known_block`, `install.sh:735-739`; see `ConsumerDoctrineBlock`), so a CRLF conversion of the file is not an edit |
| `files` | array of FileEntry | yes | One entry per file this release ships to that root (pack files only for installed packs, see `packs`), plus any dropped file that is kept and stays tracked (see lifecycle table); may be `[]`. Sorted by path in byte order (`LC_ALL=C sort` of the plan, `install.sh:1071`, entries appended in plan order; `Get-SortedKeys` with `StringComparer::Ordinal`, `install.ps1:314-318`, `install.ps1:332`) |

**Pack evidence when no root has a `packs` key** (or always, for `ondisk` only, in `install.sh --codex --project`; header comment `install.sh:1299-1314`). Each `InstallPackList` entry maps to a path per root kind (`pack_path`, `install.sh:1338-1348`): Claude `skills/<n>` → prefix `skills/<n>/`, `agents/<n>.md` → `agents/<n>.md`; Codex skills root `skills/<n>` → prefix `<n>/` (agents have no path there); Codex config root `agents/<n>.md` → `agents/<n>.toml` (skills have no path there).

- **`tracked`** — `pack_tracked <kind> <root> <list>` (`install.sh:1350-1363`), used only for a root whose manifest exists and only outside project scope: fails for a missing or symlinked manifest (`install.sh:1353`; the sync refuses a symlinked one later), else succeeds when the manifest's `files` (via `read_manifest`, `install.sh:1354`) track a path equal to an entry's path, or under its `/`-ending prefix (`install.sh:1355-1361`). ps1 `Get-PackEvidence` (`install.ps1:886-901`, comment `install.ps1:879-885`): a reparse-point manifest gives `''` (`install.ps1:888`); an existing manifest gives `'tracked'` when `Read-Manifest` keys (`install.ps1:890`) start with `skills/<n>/` (`StartsWith(…, Ordinal)`) or equal an agent entry (`-ccontains`), else `''`; with a manifest present it never looks at the disk (`install.ps1:889-897`).
- **`ondisk`** — `pack_shipped_file <kind> <root> <list>` (`install.sh:1423-1429`), used for a root with **no** manifest, and in `--codex --project` for every root. It is a wrapper: it calls `pack_shipped_files <kind> <root> <list> first` and prints only the `<rel>` part (before the TAB) of the one line that returns, failing when that call fails (`install.sh:1427-1428`). `pack_shipped_files` (`install.sh:1373-1421`, comment `install.sh:1365-1372`) prints `<rel>\t<sha>` (root-relative path, TAB, 64-hex sha256) for **every** AgenticOS copy of a file of the list. The 4th argument selects a mode: `first` prints only the first copy and fails when there is none (`install.sh:1414`); `all` also prints `<rel>\t-` (a literal `-` in place of the sha) for every other regular file reached through no symlink, i.e. an edit or the user's own file (`elif [[ "$first" == all ]]`, `install.sh:1415-1417`); with no 4th argument (or `all`) it always succeeds (`[[ "$first" != first ]]`, `install.sh:1420`). Only `pack_adopt` uses `all`. A Codex config root never counts (`*) return 1`, `install.sh:1378`; its agent TOML is generated, and every pack has skills under the skills root, comment `install.sh:1371-1372`). For each entry, the entry's path (trailing `/` dropped) must be reached through **no** symlink component, the leaf included, and must exist (`install.sh:1380-1396`). A symlinked path is never followed or deleted; in `all` mode it is named on stderr, `skip <path> — a symlink is on the way to it; symlink, left in place (AgenticOS never follows or deletes a symlink)` (`install.sh:1389-1395`). Also in `all` mode only, every symlink inside an existing pack path (`find "$root/$p" -type l -print0`) is named on stderr, `skip <rel> — symlink, left in place (AgenticOS never follows or deletes a symlink)`, and left alone (`install.sh:1397-1401`). Both names are `printf '%q'`-escaped. Then each regular file under the path (`find -type f -print0`, which does not follow links; `install.sh:1402-1418`) is checked: a root-relative `<rel>` that does not match `REL_RE` (`^[A-Za-z0-9._@+-]+(/[A-Za-z0-9._@+-]+)+$`, two or more `safe_rel`-charset components, `install.sh:424-427`) is skipped with `skip <%q rel> — unusual characters in the name; not an AgenticOS file, not touched` on stderr (stdout carries the rows), so a name with a tab or newline can never forge a `<rel>\t<sha>` row (`install.sh:1404-1408`). Every other file is hashed, and a file whose `<rel>\t<sha>` is in the earlier-release list from `load_known` (`skills/` prefix for Claude, `""` for the Codex skills root, into `$WORK/pack.known`, `install.sh:1376-1377`; see `ReleaseHashesFile`), **or** whose sha equals this release's source copy (`$SRC/<rel>`, or `$SRC/skills/<rel>` for the Codex skills root, itself a regular non-link file), is an AgenticOS copy and is printed with its sha (`install.sh:1409-1414`). So an empty pack dir, a symlink (even to a shipped copy) or the user's own skill of the same name is no evidence (comment `install.sh:1370-1372`). ps1 `Get-PackEvidence` with no manifest calls `Get-PackShippedFiles $Entries -First` and returns `"disk:<rel>"` for its first hit, else `''` (`install.ps1:898-900`). `Get-PackShippedFiles($Entries, [switch]$First, [switch]$All)` (`install.ps1:909-959`, comment `install.ps1:903-908`) is the ps1 `pack_shipped_files`: it returns one `[pscustomobject]@{ Rel = $rel; Sha = $sha }` per AgenticOS copy (`install.ps1:951`), stopping after the first with `-First` (`install.ps1:952`); with `-All` it also returns `[pscustomobject]@{ Rel = $rel; Sha = $null }` for every other regular file reached through no reparse point (an edit, or the user's own; `install.ps1:953-954`); otherwise all hits (`install.ps1:958`; an empty array when none). Same component-wise reparse-point check (`install.ps1:912-913`); a linked entry is skipped, and with `-All` named by `Warn "skip <entry> — a symlink is on the way to it; symlink, left in place (AgenticOS never follows or deletes a symlink)"` (`install.ps1:914-918`). Files are collected by a manual walk (`install.ps1:920-937`): for a directory, a `System.Collections.Stack` of dirs is popped and each child from `Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath <dir> -Force` that is a reparse point is skipped (with `-All`, `Warn "skip <child path relative to $Root, \ shown as /> — symlink, left in place (AgenticOS never follows or deletes a symlink)"`, `install.ps1:928-931`), a container is pushed, anything else is a file (`install.ps1:924-934`), so the walk never enters a junction or directory symlink (comment `install.ps1:920-921`: `Get-ChildItem -Recurse` follows them in Windows PowerShell 5.1); for an agent file, `Get-Item` of the file itself (`install.ps1:936`). Reparse-point files are skipped again (`install.ps1:940`), `<rel>` is the path below `Convert-Path $Root` with `\` turned into `/` (`install.ps1:938`, `install.ps1:941`), and a `<rel>` that fails `\A[A-Za-z0-9._@+-]+(/[A-Za-z0-9._@+-]+)+\z` (the `Test-SafeRel` charset, `\A…\z` because .NET `$` also matches before a final newline) is skipped with `Warn "skip <rel> — unusual characters in the name; not an AgenticOS file, not touched"`, so it never becomes a key (`install.ps1:942-947`). In all three ps1 warnings C0, DEL and C1 control characters (`[\x00-\x1f\x7f-\x9f]`, so also U+009B CSI) are shown as `?` (`install.ps1:916`, `install.ps1:929`, `install.ps1:945`). A file whose `` "<rel>`t<sha>" `` (TAB-joined) is in `$script:KnownHashes` or whose sha equals the leaf, non-reparse `Join-Rel $Src <rel>` is an AgenticOS copy (`install.ps1:948-950`). UNVERIFIED: the ps1 walk and its warnings were not executed for this catalog (no `pwsh`).

**Pre-manifest `--without`** (both installers; the ps1 mirror is the last bullet below). `pack_adopt <pack> <kind:root>…` (`install.sh:1450-1473`, comment `install.sh:1441-1449`) runs from `pack_plan` only for a pack that ends up not installed **because** of `--without-<pack>` (`install.sh:1527-1529`), on install, update and dry run, in any scope. For each root of the run whose manifest does not exist (neither a file nor a symlink, `install.sh:1455`; a root with a manifest is skipped, because its tracked files already take the "in manifest, not in release" rows):

- **Claude root / Codex skills root** — runs `pack_shipped_files <kind> <root> <pack list> all` into the transient scan file `$WORK/pack-adopt.scan` (stdout only, so the `skip …` warnings for symlinks and odd names go to the terminal, never into the file; overwritten per root, `install.sh:1466`), then appends only its matched lines (`LC_ALL=C awk -F'\t' '$2 != "-"'`: every AgenticOS copy, `<rel>\t<sha>` one per line) to the work file `$WORK/pack-adopt.<kind>` (`install.sh:1467`), where `<kind>` is `claude` or `codex-skills`, i.e. the `sync_place` id of that root (`CLAUDE_ARGS`, `install.sh:1717`; `SKILLS_ARGS`, `install.sh:1753`). Several `--without` packs append to the same file. For each unmatched line (`<rel>\t-`: a regular file in the pack's dirs, reached through no symlink, that is not an AgenticOS release copy) whose `<rel>` also matches `REL_RE` (`[[ "$n" == "-" && "$e" =~ $REL_RE ]]`) it prints the `warn` `keep <rel> — in the <pack> pack's dirs but not an AgenticOS release copy (edited, or yours), and no manifest tracks it; left in place, remove it by hand if you no longer want it` (`install.sh:1468-1471`). Nothing is deleted for those lines and they are never written to `$WORK/pack-adopt.<kind>`. The `warn` bumps `N_WARN` only, not `N_KEPT` (`install.sh:392`). `sync_place` re-validates every line of `$WORK/pack-adopt.<kind>` before using it (see the work-file paragraph below).
- **Codex config root** — nothing is written; for each `agents/<n>.md` entry of the pack whose `<root>/agents/<n>.toml` is a regular non-link file, it prints the note `left in place: <root>/agents/<n>.toml — no manifest says AgenticOS wrote it; delete it by hand if it is the <pack> pack's agent` (`install.sh:1456-1464`), because generated TOML cannot be hash-matched. The file is not touched.
- **`install.ps1` (Claude root only)** — in `Set-PackPlan`'s not-installed branch, after the `-Without<Pack>` note and only when `Join-Path $Root $ManifestName` neither exists nor is a reparse point (`install.ps1:988-989`), it walks `Get-PackShippedFiles $entries -All` (`install.ps1:990`; with `-All` it also names symlinked paths, reparse-point children and odd names, see "Pack evidence" above): each hit with a sha is stored as `$script:PackAdopt[$hit.Rel] = $hit.Sha` (`install.ps1:991`), and each hit with `Sha = $null` gets `Warn "keep <rel> — in the <p> pack's dirs but not an AgenticOS release copy (edited, or yours), and no manifest tracks it; left in place, remove it by hand if you no longer want it"` (`install.ps1:992`; same text as `install.sh`, not counted in `$script:Kept`) and is not adopted or deleted (comment `install.ps1:985-987`). `$script:PackAdopt` is a transient in-memory hashtable, rel → sha, initialised empty at `install.ps1:849`, never persisted. Right after `$old = Read-Manifest $Root` the sync adds each entry whose key matches `\A[A-Za-z0-9._@+-]+(/[A-Za-z0-9._@+-]+)+\z`, whose value matches `\A[0-9a-f]{64}\z` and whose rel is not already a key of `$old`; an entry failing either check is dropped with `Warn "skip a malformed pack adoption entry <key, C0/DEL/C1 controls as ?>; nothing adopted or removed for it"` (`install.ps1:1068-1071`, warning `install.ps1:1070`). So the adopted paths join the plan (`$all`, `install.ps1:1074`) and, being unstaged (`$script:PacksOff`, `install.ps1:1035-1038`), take the "no longer shipped" row: removed when `Test-Unmodified` (`install.ps1:1089`), else kept with `keep … no longer shipped, but modified locally` and recorded with the adopted sha (`install.ps1:1090-1094`). `Test-SafeRel` is checked in the plan loop (`install.ps1:1081`) and again before each `Remove-Item` (`install.ps1:1185`). The adopted rels are also keys of `$old`, which the tombstone scan uses to tell tracked from legacy files (`install.ps1:1132`). ps1 is Claude-only, so there is no Codex config-root note. UNVERIFIED: the ps1 path was not executed for this catalog (no `pwsh`).

**`$WORK/pack-adopt.<id>` work file** (transient, inside the run's `WORK` dir, removed with it by `cleanup`; never persisted, never in a manifest). Format: the same `<rel>\t<sha>` lines `read_manifest` prints. `sync_place` reads the manifest into `$w/old.tsv` (`install.sh:1043`), and when `$WORK/pack-adopt.$id` is non-empty re-validates it line by line (`install.sh:1044-1058`): each whole line (`IFS= read -r line`) is split at its first TAB into `a_rel` / `a_sha`, and it is accepted only when the line holds a TAB, `a_sha` holds no second TAB, `a_sha` matches `SHA_RE` (`^[0-9a-f]{64}$`, `install.sh:428`) and `row_ok "$a_rel"` passes (`a_rel` matches `REL_RE`; `row_ok`, `install.sh:436-439`, checks only its first argument). Accepted lines are rewritten as `<rel>\t<sha>` into the new transient `$w/adopt.ok`; any other line gets `warn` `skip a malformed pack adoption row <line, printf %q>; nothing adopted or removed for it` and is dropped (`install.sh:1049-1056`). Then `LC_ALL=C sort -u "$w/old.tsv" "$w/adopt.ok"` replaces `old.tsv` (`install.sh:1057`). The adopted paths are then treated exactly as if an old manifest tracked them at the sha they had when `pack_adopt` hashed them: they are not staged (`unstage_packs`), so they take the "in manifest, not in release" rows of the lifecycle table: each must still pass `safe_rel` in the plan loop (else it is skipped as unsafe) (`install.sh:1080-1083`) and is removed as `no longer shipped` only when `unmodified` (current sha equals the adopted sha, or a known earlier-release copy, `install.sh:1097-1098`), with `safe_rel` re-checked right before the `rm` in `sync_finish` (`install.sh:1203-1206`). A file that matched no AgenticOS copy (an edit, the user's own file in a pack dir) is never listed and is not touched; `pack_adopt` names it with the `keep … left in place, remove it by hand …` warning above. The intermediate `$WORK/pack-adopt.scan` (all `pack_shipped_files … all` lines of the current root, matched and `<rel>\t-`) is overwritten per root, lives in the same `WORK` dir and is read only by `pack_adopt`. A listed file that changed between `pack_adopt` and `sync_place` takes the "modified" row: kept with `keep … no longer shipped, but modified locally` and recorded in the new manifest with the adopted sha. In a dry run the removals are only reported. The adopted paths also join `old.paths`, which the retired-skill (tombstone) scan uses to tell tracked from legacy files (`install.sh:1059`, `install.sh:1162`). Exercised by `scripts/install-packs-test.sh` (Claude `install-packs-test.sh:268-289`, including the removal of the unedited `agents/marketer.md` at `install-packs-test.sh:276-278` and the `⚠ keep <f> — in the marketing pack's dirs …` check for an edited skill file and a user file at `install-packs-test.sh:282-285`; an edited pack agent kept and named while its unedited sibling is removed, `install-packs-test.sh:291-301`; a symlinked pack skill dir and a symlinked file in a pack dir named, neither followed nor deleted, `install-packs-test.sh:303-318`; Codex skills root plus the config-root note `install-packs-test.sh:320-335`; a newline-in-dir-name plus tab-in-file-name forged `<rel>\t<sha>` row, with and without the victim's sha, never adopts or removes the user's `agents/my-private.md` on either run, `install-packs-test.sh:337-362`; control characters in pack-dir names printed only escaped, `install-packs-test.sh:364-375`). Round 5 adds direct row-check cases (`install-packs-test.sh:578-637`): `seam_installer` (`install-packs-test.sh:583-589`) runs a COPY of the installer with one line inserted after an exact anchor (a test seam in the copy only) that plants a malformed row for a canary `agents/my-private.md` (`canary_home`, `install-packs-test.sh:590-595`): A, a `$WORK/pack-adopt.claude` row with an extra field (`skip a malformed pack adoption row`, `install-packs-test.sh:597-604`); B, two `sync_place` `plan.tsv` rows (extra field, collapsed empty sha), each reported `skip malformed plan row agents/my-private.md` and the canary neither removed nor tracked (`install-packs-test.sh:606-614`); C, the same two shapes in `uninstall_root`'s `plan.tsv` (`install-packs-test.sh:616-624`); D, a newline in a retired skill's file name cannot forge a `$WORK/unsafe.lst` line naming a shipped hook, so `--dry-run` still passes and the name is reported `%q`-escaped (`install-packs-test.sh:626-637`). Read, not run, for this catalog.

**Doctrine record read rules** (not part of the `files` parse filters below):

- `install.sh` `read_doctrine_record` (`install.sh:688-692`): returns nothing when the manifest is missing or a symlink (`install.sh:690`). Otherwise a line-wise `sed -nE` regex `^  "doctrine": \{"created": (true|false), "block_sha256": "([0-9a-f]{64})"\},$` prints `<created> <sha>` for the first matching line (`install.sh:691`). It depends on the exact two-space indent, spacing and trailing comma above; any other form is read as no record. Called on `$DOC_MF_ROOT/$MANIFEST_NAME` (`install.sh:1643`), split into `DOC_OLD_CREATED` / `DOC_OLD_SHA` (`install.sh:1644`). Then `DOC_PROJECT` is set to `false` and `DOC_NOREC_WHY` to `""` (`install.sh:1645-1646`). For `--codex --project` (`PLATFORM == codex` and `CODEX_SCOPE == project`) the record is treated as absent: `DOC_PROJECT=true; DOC_OLD_SHA=""; DOC_OLD_CREATED=false`, and `DOC_NOREC_WHY` becomes ` AgenticOS can trust (in --codex --project the manifest is repository content)` (`install.sh:1647-1655`), because the repository can commit `.codex/.agentic-os-manifest.json`. The comment there states that no doctrine record is written in project scope (`write_manifest`, `install.sh:1648-1652`). `DOC_NOREC_WHY` is appended after `no install record` in the `--with-doctrine` foreign refusal (`install.sh:788`) and the uninstall hands-off reason (`install.sh:815`). `install.ps1` is Claude-only and has no project scope, so it has no equivalent.
- `install.ps1` `Read-DoctrineRecord` (`install.ps1:584-593`): `$null` when the manifest is a reparse point or not a file (`install.ps1:586`); else `ConvertFrom-Json` (`install.ps1:587`), and the record counts only when `doctrine.created` is a `[bool]` and `doctrine.block_sha256` is a string matching `^[0-9a-f]{64}$` (case-sensitive, `install.ps1:589`). Malformed JSON throws (`$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"`, `install.ps1:56`).
- The `files` readers ignore the `packs` and `doctrine` lines: `read_manifest` matches only `"path": …, "sha256": …` lines (`install.sh:415`) and `Read-Manifest` reads only `$json.files` (`install.ps1:306`). Likewise `read_doctrine_record` ignores the `packs` line and `read_packs_record` the `doctrine` line. Like the project-scope doctrine record, a `packs` record in a `--codex --project` manifest (repository content) is **not** trusted: `pack_plan` never reads it there, nor the files that manifest tracks (`proj=true`, `install.sh:1483-1491`, `install.sh:1507`), yet still writes a fresh record (see `packs`). The `$WORK/pack-adopt.<id>` lines that `sync_place` merges into `old.tsv` (see "Pre-manifest `--without`") and the `$script:PackAdopt` entries ps1 merges into `$old` are not read from any manifest; they only exist for a root with no manifest.

FileEntry (one per line, exactly `{"path": "<rel>", "sha256": "<hex>"}` — `install.sh:466`, `install.ps1:335`):

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `path` | string | yes | Relative to **that** root, `/`-separated, no leading `./` (`install.sh:405`, `install.ps1:293`). Claude root: `skills/…`, `agents/…`, `commands/…`, `hooks/…`, and `agentic-os/doctrine.md` while the doctrine mode is `write` or `handsoff` (`ConsumerDoctrineFile` staged at `install.sh:1707-1711`, `install.ps1:1049-1052`); Codex skills root: `<skill>/…` (no `skills/` prefix); Codex config root: `agents/<name>.toml`, `hooks/…` (`install.sh:1591`, `install.sh:1743-1756`). Skills and agents of a pack that is not installed are not staged, so they are not listed (see `packs`). Codex inlines the doctrine into `AGENTS.md`, so no Codex root tracks a doctrine file. Release paths are restricted to `[A-Za-z0-9._@+-]` segments, else the install aborts (`install.sh:1031-1035`, `install.ps1:1059-1061`), so no JSON escaping is done (`install.sh:441-442`). Only paths that pass `safe_rel` / `Test-SafeRel` are recorded (`install.sh:1080-1083`, `install.ps1:1081`) |
| `sha256` | string (64 hex, lowercase) | yes | SHA-256 of the **release copy** of the file (`nsha`, `install.sh:1106`; `$nsha`, `install.ps1:1097-1098`) — also for an in-release file kept because the user modified it, so a later run still sees it as modified. For a file the release dropped but that is kept (modified locally, or a `jq`-less hook), the **old** manifest sha `o` is carried forward (`install.sh:1095`, `install.sh:1101`; `install.ps1:1092`); for a pre-manifest pack file adopted through `$WORK/pack-adopt.<id>`, `o` is the sha `pack_adopt` hashed (ps1: the `$script:PackAdopt` sha from `Get-PackShippedFiles`, `install.ps1:1069-1071`). Compared on update, prune and uninstall against the on-disk sha **and** against `ReleaseHashesFile` (see "Unmodified" below), so a file whose content matches an earlier release's copy of that path counts as unmodified even when it does not match `o` |

**Read filters** (parse filters only — an entry failing one is silently ignored; containment is **not** enforced here but by the path-safety gate below — `install.sh:409-411`, `install.ps1:299-300`):

- `install.sh` `read_manifest` (`install.sh:412-422`) is a line-wise `awk` regex, **not** a JSON parser: it matches `"path": "[^"]*", "sha256": "[0-9a-f]+"` once per line (`install.sh:415`), so it depends on the one-entry-per-line, exact-spacing form above. Drops entries whose sha is not 64 chars or whose path is outside `^[A-Za-z0-9._@+/-]+$` (`install.sh:419`); absolute and `..` paths pass this filter. Output de-duplicated (`sort -u`, `install.sh:421`). Unparseable content yields zero entries (install then treats every file as pre-manifest; uninstall removes nothing but still deletes the manifest).
- `install.ps1` `Read-Manifest` (`install.ps1:301-312`) parses with `ConvertFrom-Json` (`install.ps1:305`); keeps entries where `path` and `sha256` are strings, `sha256 -cmatch '\A[0-9a-f]{64}\z'` (case-sensitive, so uppercase hex is dropped, as in `install.sh`) and `path -cmatch '\A[A-Za-z0-9._@+/-]+\z'` (`install.ps1:307`). The `\A…\z` anchors (since round 4 of #299; formerly `^…$`) matter because .NET `$` also matches before a final newline, so a path or sha ending in `\n` is now dropped. Unlike `install.sh`, malformed JSON is not read as zero entries: `ConvertFrom-Json` throws (`$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"`, `install.ps1:56`) and the install or uninstall aborts.

**Path-safety gate** — `safe_rel <root> <rel> <allow>` (`install.sh:498-530`) / `Test-SafeRel` (`install.ps1:245-266`), checked before every write, hook backup and `rm` (`install.sh:1062`, `install.sh:1080-1083`, `install.sh:1158-1161`, `install.sh:1203-1206`, `install.sh:1261`, `install.sh:1276-1277`; `install.ps1:808`, `install.ps1:1081`, `install.ps1:1131`, `install.ps1:1185`). A path passes only if:

- every component matches `[A-Za-z0-9._@+-]` and there are **at least two** components, so nothing at the root is ever touched through this gate — not the manifest, `settings.json`, `hooks.json`, `CLAUDE.md` or `AGENTS.md` (`[[ "$rel" =~ $REL_RE ]]`, `install.sh:500`, the shared `REL_RE` at `install.sh:427`, no longer an inline copy; ps1 `install.ps1:247`, anchored `\A…\z` since round 4 of #299 because .NET `$` also matches before a final newline, comment `install.ps1:246`). The doctrine block in `CLAUDE.md` / `AGENTS.md` is written by separate code that does not use this gate (`doc_write` / `Write-BesideBytes`, planned by `doctrine_plan` / `Set-DoctrinePlan` and re-checked by `doc_recheck` / `Assert-DocUnchanged`; see `ConsumerDoctrineBlock`);
- no component is `.` or `..` (`install.sh:501`, `install.ps1:248`);
- the first component is in that root's allowlist: Claude `skills agents commands hooks agentic-os` (`CLAUDE_ALLOW`, `install.sh:1621`; `$ShippedPrefixes`, `install.ps1:217`, check `install.ps1:250`); Codex config `agents hooks` (`CODEX_CONFIG_ALLOW`, `install.sh:1628`); Codex skills root `*` = any first component (`install.sh:504-505`, passed at `install.sh:1681`, `install.sh:1753`). The former `memory` / `ledger` exclusion is gone; the skill-name rule below replaces it;
- **`agentic-os` holds one file:** a path whose first component is `agentic-os` passes only when it is exactly `agentic-os/doctrine.md` (`$DOCTRINE_REL`; `install.sh:508-509`; ps1 `-cne 'agentic-os/doctrine.md'`, `install.ps1:251-252`), so a tampered manifest cannot reach any other file under `<DEST>/agentic-os/`;
- **skill-name allowlist:** for a Claude `skills/<name>/…` path, and for every Codex skills-root path (where the first component is the skill name), `<name>` must be in `SKILL_NAMES` (`install.sh:503-514`) / `$script:SkillNames` (`install.ps1:253-254`; ps1 is Claude-only, so only `skills/<name>`). The set is the union of the tombstoned slugs, the skill directories in this release's `$SRC/skills` (pack skills included, whether or not the pack is installed), and every `skills/<name>/` in `ReleaseHashesFile` (`install.sh:1624-1627`; `install.ps1:358`, `install.ps1:364`, `install.ps1:368-369`). A tampered manifest therefore cannot reach another vendor's skills;
- no component from the root down to the file, the leaf included, is a symlink (`[[ -L ]]`, which also sees a dangling link, `install.sh:515-520`) — in ps1, a reparse point on **every** component, including ones that do not exist yet: `Test-ReparsePoint` reads `FileInfo.Attributes`, so a dangling link is still seen, and `-1` means nothing exists there (`install.ps1:219-224`, loop `install.ps1:257-261`);
- containment: `install.sh` — when the parent dir exists, its `pwd -P` lies inside the canonical root `ROOT_CANON` (set by `set_root`, `install.sh:480-484`; check `install.sh:521-528`); `install.ps1` — `[System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath` of the joined path must start with `GetFullPath($Root)` plus a separator (`OrdinalIgnoreCase`, `install.ps1:262-265`).

A failing entry is skipped with `skip <rel> — unsafe path (outside the shipped dirs, unusual characters, or through a symlink); not touched` (`unsafe`, `install.sh:567-571`; `Skip-Unsafe`, `install.ps1:268-273`) and is **not** re-recorded in the new manifest (`install.sh:1080-1083`, `install.ps1:1081`). Each skip also increments the unsafe counter (`N_UNSAFE`, `install.sh:388`, `install.sh:569`; `$script:Unsafe`, `install.ps1:201`, `install.ps1:271`) and records the path for the dry-run hook check (`$WORK/unsafe.lst`, `install.sh:570`; `$script:UnsafeRels`, `install.ps1:203`, `install.ps1:272`). `install.sh` appends to `$WORK/unsafe.lst` only a `<rel>` matching `REL_RE`, one per line (`install.sh:570`), so a newline in a name can no longer forge a line naming a shipped hook and fail `--dry-run`'s `verify_hooks`; any other name is still warned (`printf '%q'`-escaped, `install.sh:568`) and counted, just not listed. ps1 `Skip-Unsafe` prints the path with C0, DEL and C1 controls (`[\x00-\x1f\x7f-\x9f]`) replaced by `?` (`install.ps1:269`) and adds the raw `$Rel` to the in-memory set. On uninstall it is likewise skipped, and the manifest is still deleted (`install.sh:1276-1277`, `install.sh:1294-1295`; `install.ps1:808`, `install.ps1:825-826`).

**Unmodified** — a file is an AgenticOS copy when its on-disk sha `c` equals the manifest sha `o`, **or** equals any earlier release's sha for that same path in `ReleaseHashesFile` (`unmodified`, `install.sh:546-552`, known list from `load_known` `install.sh:532-544`; `Test-Unmodified`, `install.ps1:371-377`, a `$null` `c` is never unmodified, `install.ps1:374`). Used for in-release updates, removal of dropped files (including the files of a pack that is not installed), and uninstall (`install.sh:1097`, `install.sh:1112`, `install.sh:1283`; `install.ps1:816`, `install.ps1:1089`, `install.ps1:1104`), and for `--prune-legacy` / `-PruneLegacy` with **no** manifest sha (`"-"` in `install.sh:1170`, `$null` in `install.ps1:1139`), so there only a known earlier-release copy of that exact path counts. So adopting a pre-manifest install (no `o`) **updates** every file that matches some earlier release; only a file matching no known copy is kept as legacy. On the Codex config root, earlier-release paths are compared as-is, so a converted `agents/<name>.toml` never matches a known copy (the list holds `agents/<name>.md`); only `hooks/…` can.

**Lifecycle semantics** (per path that passes the gate, comparing release sha `n`, manifest sha `o`, on-disk sha `c` — `sync_place`, `install.sh:1024-1193`, per-path loop `install.sh:1075-1140`; `install.ps1:1080-1121`). "In manifest" also covers a pre-manifest pack file listed in `$WORK/pack-adopt.<id>` or `$script:PackAdopt` (see "Pre-manifest `--without`"). **Plan-row check (`install.sh` only):** `$w/plan.tsv` rows are read with `IFS=$'\t' read -r rel nsha osha csha extra`, and because tab is IFS whitespace an empty field collapses, so every row is re-checked after the read: it is used only when the 5th variable `extra` is empty and `shas_ok "$nsha" "$osha" "$csha"` passes (each sha is `-` or matches `SHA_RE`; the path field is gated, and reported if refused, by `safe_rel`, not by `REL_RE`); otherwise `warn` `skip malformed plan row <rel, printf %q> — nothing written or removed for it` and the row is skipped before `safe_rel` (`install.sh:1075-1079`; `REL_RE` / `SHA_RE` / `shas_ok` / `row_ok`, `install.sh:424-439`). ps1 keeps hashtables, not TSV rows, and has no such step:

| Case | Action | Re-recorded in new manifest? |
|---|---|---|
| malformed plan row (`install.sh` only; see "Plan-row check" above) | skipped with `skip malformed plan row …` warning; nothing written or removed for it (`install.sh:1075-1079`) | no |
| fails `safe_rel` / `Test-SafeRel` | skipped with `unsafe path` warning, not touched (`install.sh:1080-1083`, `install.ps1:1081`) | no |
| in manifest, not in release (any disk state), path `hooks/<name>.sh` | basename added to the derived retired-hook list for unregistration (`install.sh:1086-1089`, `install.ps1:1087`; see `ConsumerHookRegistration`), then one of the rows below | — |
| in manifest, not in release, missing on disk | nothing (`install.sh:1090-1091`, `install.ps1:1088`) | no |
| in manifest, not in release, under `hooks/`, `jq` missing (`install.sh` only) | left in place with plain `warn` `keep retired hook <rel> — jq is required to unregister it first`; **not** counted as kept (`install.sh:1092-1096`) | yes (`o`) |
| in manifest, not in release, unmodified (`c == o` or `c` is a known earlier-release copy) | removed (`no longer shipped`) (`install.sh:1097-1098`, `install.ps1:1089`) | no |
| in manifest, not in release, modified (matches neither) | kept with warning `keep <rel> — no longer shipped, but modified locally`; stays tracked so uninstall and `--prune-legacy` keep treating it as modified (`install.sh:1099-1103`, `install.ps1:1090-1094`) | yes (`o`) |
| in release, missing on disk | added (`install.sh:1107-1109`, `install.ps1:1100-1101`) | yes (`n`) |
| `c == n` | unchanged (`install.sh:1110-1111`, `install.ps1:1102-1103`) | yes (`n`) |
| unmodified: `c == o`, or `c` is a known earlier-release copy (also with no `o`, i.e. a pre-manifest install) | updated to release copy, `update <rel>` (`install.sh:1112-1116`, `install.ps1:1104-1105`) | yes (`n`) |
| path under `hooks/`, otherwise differs | always replaced; old copy saved (not in dry run, `cp -p` / `Copy-Item`) as `<file>.bak.<YYYYmmddHHMMSS>.<pid>`, or `<file>.bak.<YYYYmmddHHMMSS>.<pid>.<n>` (n = 1, 2, …) if that name exists **or is a link, dangling included** (`STAMP`, `install.sh:383`, loop `install.sh:1119-1121` tests `-e \|\| -L`, copy `install.sh:1126`; `$Stamp`, `install.ps1:197`, loop `install.ps1:1108-1109` tests `Test-Path -LiteralPath` **or** `Test-ReparsePoint`, copy `install.ps1:1110`). `install.sh` first warns `hook <rel> was hard-linked elsewhere; the link is replaced, the other copies are not changed` when the file has more than one link (`find -links +1`, `install.sh:1122-1125`); ps1 has no such warning | yes (`n`) |
| `--force` / `-Force` | overwritten (`install.sh:1130-1133`, `install.ps1:1114-1115`) | yes (`n`) |
| no `o` (pre-manifest install), differs and matches no known copy | kept with warning, counted as legacy (`install.sh:1134-1136`, `install.ps1:1116-1117`) | yes (`n`) |
| `o` present, `c` differs from `n`, `o` and every known copy | kept with warning `keep <rel> — modified locally` (`install.sh:1137-1139`, `install.ps1:1118-1119`) | yes (`n`) |

`agentic-os/doctrine.md` follows these rows like any other Claude file: staged only while the doctrine mode is `write` or `handsoff` (`install.sh:1658-1659`, `install.sh:1707-1711`; `install.ps1:784`, `install.ps1:1049-1052`), so a locally edited copy is kept with `keep … modified locally` unless `--force`. In mode `optout` (the user deleted the block) it is no longer staged, so a tracked unmodified copy is removed as `no longer shipped` and a modified one is kept and stays tracked.

Pack files follow the same rows: the skills and agents of a pack that is not installed (see `packs`) are removed from the stage before `sync_place`, so on a root whose manifest tracks them they take the "in manifest, not in release" rows (`--without-<pack>`: unmodified files removed as `no longer shipped`, edited ones kept with `keep … no longer shipped, but modified locally` and re-recorded with `o`). Pack files the manifest does not track are not in the plan and are never touched, with one exception: on a root with **no** manifest, `install.sh --without-<pack>` / `install.ps1 -Without<Pack>` puts the pack's AgenticOS copies into the plan through `$WORK/pack-adopt.<id>` / `$script:PackAdopt`, so they take the same "in manifest, not in release" rows (unmodified copies removed, everything else left; see "Pre-manifest `--without`"). (When `ondisk` evidence selects a pack on a root with no manifest, its files enter the plan as ordinary "in release" rows and are adopted like any pre-manifest file.)

Every "kept" row except the `jq`-missing retired hook, plus the uninstall "modified locally" keep, goes through `kept()` / `Kept`, which warns and increments a separate kept counter (`N_KEPT`, `install.sh:387`, `install.sh:393`; `$script:Kept`, `install.ps1:200`, `install.ps1:207`). The `jq`-missing retired-hook keep, the uninstall hands-off doctrine-file keep (below), the hook-refresh and the tombstone messages use plain `warn` / `Warn` (or `note` / `Note`) and are not counted as kept. A `--prune-legacy` foreign file (see below) is counted in a separate foreign counter (`N_FOREIGN`, `install.sh:389`, `install.sh:1179`; `$script:Foreign`, `install.ps1:202`, `install.ps1:1144`). The closing summary prints each count on its own line, only when non-zero: `<N> file(s) you modified were kept; --force overwrites them.`, `<N> file(s) in retired skill directories are not AgenticOS release files and were left alone; they are yours to keep or remove.` and `<N> path(s) were skipped as unsafe and left untouched (see the 'skip' lines above).` (`install.sh:1782-1790`; ps1 uses `-Force`, `install.ps1:1227-1229`). Uninstall prints `Uninstall complete: <kept> modified file(s) kept, <unsafe> unsafe path(s) skipped. …` (`install.sh:1685`, `install.ps1:832`).

**Hook verification before registration** — `verify_hooks <stage> <root>` (`install.sh:623-644`) runs after `sync_place` and before the hook merge and `sync_finish` (Claude `install.sh:1718-1730`; Codex config root `install.sh:1755-1770`). For every `hooks/*` file in the staged release: in a real run the installed file must exist as a regular file (`-f`, not `-L`) with the same sha as the staged copy; in a dry run it must not be in `$WORK/unsafe.lst` (`install.sh:625-632`), which holds only `REL_RE`-matching names, one per line (`unsafe`, `install.sh:570`), so no file name can forge a hook line there. Each miss prints `✗ hook <rel> is not installed at the release version` (dry run: `✗ hook <rel> would be skipped`), and any miss prints `Error: not every AgenticOS hook could be installed at the release version. Files that could be placed were already copied; only hook registration and the manifest update were withheld (planned removals were skipped too). Fix the paths reported above, then re-run to finish.` and exits 1 (`install.sh:633-636`). ps1 does the same in the `$badHooks` block (`install.ps1:1157-1167`: leaf, no reparse point, `Get-Sha` equals `$new[$rel]`; dry run: in `$script:UnsafeRels`), printing `X hook <rel> is not installed at the release version` (`install.ps1:1164`) and throwing the same error text (`install.ps1:1166`) before `Update-ClaudeHookSettings "merge"` (`install.ps1:1174`) and `Write-Manifest` (`install.ps1:1191`). On failure the files that `sync_place` / the ps1 loop already placed stay as placed; only hook registration, the manifest write and the planned removals (dropped files, `--prune-legacy`) are withheld. Re-running after fixing the reported paths finishes the install.

**Doctrine file verification (Claude, doctrine mode `write`, real run)** — after `verify_hooks` and before the hook merge, `<DEST>/agentic-os/doctrine.md` must be a regular file and not a symlink, else `Error: <DEST>/agentic-os/doctrine.md could not be installed, so CLAUDE.md was not given an import of it; hook registration and the manifest update were withheld. Fix the path reported above, then re-run.` and exit 1 (`install.sh:1720-1723`; ps1 same text with `$docFile`, `install.ps1:1169-1173`). So the `CLAUDE.md` block is never written without the imported file on disk. Mode `handsoff` skips this check (the target is not touched).

**File writes:** every add / update / overwrite / hook refresh goes through `place()` (`install.sh:556-565`): `mkdir -p` the parent, `cp -p` the release file to `mktemp "$dir/.agentic-os.XXXXXX"` beside the target, then `mv -f` over it, so a hard link or symlink at the target is replaced, never written through; a no-op in dry run. The temp path is held in the global `INFLIGHT` (`install.sh:227`) from `mktemp` until it is cleared after the `mv` (`install.sh:561-564`), so `cleanup` removes it when an error or signal stops the run mid-copy, a failing `cp` included (see `ConsumerDoctrineBlock` "Crash rollback"). `write_manifest` holds its `.agentic-os-manifest.XXXXXX` temp the same way (`install.sh:451`, cleared `install.sh:472`). ps1 `Copy-Placed` does the same with a `.agentic-os.<random>` temp and `Move-Item -Force` (`install.ps1:345-352`). **Executable bit:** on a real run only, after the checks pass, `verify_hooks` runs `chmod +x` on each verified shipped hook, i.e. each staged `hooks/*` path that ends in `.sh` and whose installed file has exactly one link (`find "$root/$rel" -links 1`, `install.sh:637-643`). This covers the Claude root and the Codex config root (calls `install.sh:1719`, `install.sh:1757`). There is no blanket `chmod` over `hooks/`, so a user's own files there and hard-linked hooks are never made executable. ps1 does no `chmod`.

Retired skill directories (`TOMBSTONES` from `scripts/validate.sh`, `install.sh:375`, `install.ps1:185-187`; loop `install.sh:1145-1190`, `install.ps1:1125-1151`): a tombstoned dir that this release does not ship and that exists is scanned. A dir that is itself a symlink / reparse point is skipped with a warning (`install.sh:1150-1153`, `install.ps1:1128`). Each file must lie under the tombstoned dir and pass `safe_rel` / `Test-SafeRel`, else it is skipped and reported as unsafe (`install.sh:1158-1161`, `install.ps1:1131`). The legacy list is the files the old manifest does not track (`install.sh:1162`, `install.ps1:1132`). Each legacy file is then classified (`install.sh:1168-1182`, `install.ps1:1137-1146`): **prunable** when its on-disk sha matches a known earlier-release copy of that exact path (`unmodified` against `known.tsv` with manifest sha `"-"`, `install.sh:1170`; `Test-Unmodified … $null`, `install.ps1:1139`), else **foreign** (user notes, edits, another tool's file of the same name).

- **With `--prune-legacy` / `-PruneLegacy`:** each prunable file is queued for removal with reason `retired skill <t> (--prune-legacy)` / `(-PruneLegacy)` (`install.sh:1172-1174`, `install.ps1:1141`). Each foreign file gets a `warn` `keep <rel> — not an AgenticOS release file (retired skill <t>); it is yours to keep or remove` and increments `N_FOREIGN` / `$script:Foreign`, **not** `N_KEPT` (`install.sh:1177-1180`, `install.ps1:1144`).
- **Without the flag:** nothing is removed. When prunable > 0, one `warn` says `retired skill directory <d> still holds <prunable> AgenticOS file(s) from an earlier release — re-run with --prune-legacy to remove them` (`install.sh:1184-1185`; ps1 `skills/<t>` and `-PruneLegacy`, `install.ps1:1148`). When only foreign files remain, a `note` (not a warning) says `retired skill directory <d> holds only files that are not AgenticOS release files; they are yours to keep or remove` (`install.sh:1186-1187`, `install.ps1:1149`). Foreign files are not counted in this mode.

Removal deletes files only and then prunes emptied dirs, so the dir stays whenever any file is kept (`install.sh:1207-1211`, `install.ps1:1186-1190`).
