(5) `anthropic-beta value(s) not allowed: <bad values, comma-joined, cut to 120>` (400) (`api-proxy.mjs:300-305`; tested `api-proxy-test.sh:130`). (6) `body too large` (413) past 32 MiB (`api-proxy.mjs:308`). (7) **New:** `invalid request (validator error)` (400) when `validate` throws (`api-proxy.mjs:312`). (8) Validation (400, `validate`, `api-proxy.mjs:145-172`; `GET /v1/models` skips it), in this order: `body is not JSON`, `body is not an object`, **`request field not allowed: <key, cut to 40>`** (new), `model not on the allowlist: <model, cut to 60>`, `max_tokens must be 1..<PROXY_MAX_TOKENS>` (`/v1/messages` only), then the tool checks `tools must be an array`, `tool is not an object`, `tool type not allowed: <type, cut to 60>`, `tool key not allowed: <key, cut to 40>`, `tool needs name and an object input_schema`, then `messages must be an array`, `message is not an object`, and the block walk over messages, then over `system`: **`content nested deeper than 32`** (new), `<message\|system\|tool_result> content must be a string or an array`, `<…> block is not an object`, `content block type not allowed: <type, cut to 40>`, `content source type not allowed: <type, cut to 40>` (`api-proxy.mjs:148-170`). **Changed:** the tool checks now run before the messages; `mcp_servers not allowed`, `container not allowed` and `service_tier not allowed: …` are gone (the field allowlist covers them). (9) `proxy is shutting down` (503, `cause:"drain"`) if draining began while the body was read (`api-proxy.mjs:314`). (10) **Changed in round 9 (moved first, text changed):** `unit budget exhausted: cap <PROXY_MAX_UNITS> units (committed <c> + this <r>)` (429, `cause:"budget"`) (`api-proxy.mjs:225-227`). The round-8 text's `+ in flight <f>` part is gone. (11) `proxy concurrency: <PROXY_MAX_INFLIGHT> requests already in flight` (429, `cause:"inflight"`) (`api-proxy.mjs:228`). (11b) **New in round 9:** `proxy concurrency: in-flight reservations (<f>) + committed (<c>) + this (<r>) exceed cap <PROXY_MAX_UNITS> for now` (429, `cause:"inflight"`) (`api-proxy.mjs:229-231`). (12) **Moved in round 8:** `request cap <N> reached` (429, no `cause`, so it counts in `denials`), `N` = `PROXY_MAX_REQUESTS`. The counter is bumped by this check, which only admitted requests reach (`api-proxy.mjs:232-234`). The round-7 `proxy cap/concurrency: …` texts and the round-6 `queue full (…)`, `queue wait over … ms` and `token cap … reached (committed … + reserved …)` texts are gone. Tested: `api-proxy-test.sh:119-139`, `api-proxy-test.sh:179-233`; docker `run-release-arms-docker-test.sh:110-115` |
| `cause` | string | some denial lines | `inflight` \| `budget` \| `drain` (`api-proxy.mjs:54`, `api-proxy.mjs:109-116`). **Changed in round 9:** `budget` only on the committed-plus-this check (`api-proxy.mjs:225-227`). `inflight` is on the in-flight-limit 429 (`api-proxy.mjs:228`) and, new, on the in-flight-spike 429 (`api-proxy.mjs:229-231`), which was `budget` in round 8. In round 8 these two causes replaced the round-7 `cap`; `drain` on the two `proxy is shutting down` 503s (`api-proxy.mjs:296`, `api-proxy.mjs:314`). An `inflight` line counts in `inflight_rejected`, a `budget` line in `budget_rejected` and a `drain` line in `drain_rejected`, none in `denials`, both here and in `ReleaseArmSampleRow.proxy` (`sample-row.mjs:83-86`). Downstream only `budget` (and `drain`) is a stop signal (`sample-row.mjs:116`, `sample-row.mjs:118-123`) |
| `summary` | `true` | final line only | Written after the drain on SIGTERM or SIGINT (`api-proxy.mjs:340-342`). Missing when the proxy was killed (for example past `docker stop -t 30`), crashed, or never started; the row is then incomplete with `no proxy summary` (`sample-row.mjs:107`) |
| `signal` | string | summary line only | `SIGTERM` \| `SIGINT` (`api-proxy.mjs:347-348`) |
| `requests` | integer | summary line only | Requests admitted past the three admission checks and forwarded or echoed (`S.requests++`, `api-proxy.mjs:236`). That is one per `start` line, including ones that later failed, were aborted or were unfinished. It is not the request-cap counter `count`, which since round 8 is bumped once per request that passes the three admission checks, so it also counts one then denied by the request cap (`api-proxy.mjs:234`) |
| `denials` | integer | summary line only | **Changed.** Number of `deny` calls with **no** `cause` (`api-proxy.mjs:115`): the route, key, request-cap, beta, body-size, validator and validation denials |
| `inflight_rejected` | integer | summary line only | **New in round 8** (with `budget_rejected`, replaces `cap_rejected`). Number of `deny` calls with `cause:"inflight"` (`api-proxy.mjs:112`, `api-proxy.mjs:340`) |
| `budget_rejected` | integer | summary line only | **New in round 8.** Number of `deny` calls with `cause:"budget"` (`api-proxy.mjs:113`, `api-proxy.mjs:340`) |
| `drain_rejected` | integer | summary line only | Number of `deny` calls with `cause:"drain"` (`api-proxy.mjs:114`, `api-proxy.mjs:341`) |
| `client_aborted` | integer | summary line only | In-flight requests whose client disconnected (`api-proxy.mjs:258`, `api-proxy.mjs:341`) |
| `max_inflight_seen` | integer | summary line only | **New.** The largest number of requests in flight at once, `0` when none was admitted (`api-proxy.mjs:238`, `api-proxy.mjs:341`) |
| `committed_units` | number | summary line only | Final `S.committed`, including worst-case and unfinished charges (`api-proxy.mjs:342`) |
| `unparsed` | integer | summary line only | `messages` requests charged their reservation without usage: a 2xx reply with no parsable usage, or a failure after the body was written (client abort included) (`api-proxy.mjs:250-251`) |
| `unfinished` | integer | summary line only | Requests still in flight when the drain timed out; **changed:** can now be more than 1 (`api-proxy.mjs:333-338`) |
| `by_model` | object | summary line only | `{<model>: {input, cache_read, cache_creation, output}}` summed over every charge, worst-case and unfinished charges included (`charge`, `api-proxy.mjs:212-218`; `api-proxy.mjs:252`, `api-proxy.mjs:336`). A model appears only once something was charged to it; `count_tokens` and `GET /v1/models` never add to it. So these are **charged** tokens, which equal billed tokens only when `unparsed` and `unfinished` are 0. Tested: `api-proxy-test.sh:145-146` |

Removed in round 8: the summary's `cap_rejected`, `cause:"cap"`, and the `proxy cap/concurrency: …` denial texts. Removed in round 7: the `queue_wait_ms` field on `start` lines, the `{dropped, path, queue_wait_ms}` line, and the summary's `dropped`, `queue_wait_ms_total` and `queue_wait_ms_max`. A request that is not admitted is answered at once; nothing waits and nothing is dropped.

Never logged: headers, bodies, query strings, the key (`api-proxy.mjs:49`, `api-proxy.mjs:104`, `api-proxy.mjs:116`). Upstream request headers: `content-length` = the forwarded body length and `accept-encoding: identity` (`api-proxy.mjs:315`); then every client header not in `DROP` (`host`, `x-api-key`, `authorization`, `connection`, `keep-alive`, `proxy-authorization`, `proxy-connection`, `te`, `trailer`, `upgrade`, `transfer-encoding`, `content-length`, `accept-encoding`, `x-forwarded-for`, `x-forwarded-host`, `x-forwarded-proto`, `forwarded`; `api-proxy.mjs:97-99`, `api-proxy.mjs:316`); then `x-api-key` = the proxy's key (`api-proxy.mjs:317`). The query string is still forwarded upstream (`api-proxy.mjs:272`). Because the file is `docker logs ... 2>&1`, non-JSON lines can also land in it: a node crash trace, or docker's error text when the container does not exist (`run-release-arms.sh:581`). `sample-row.mjs` skips them (`sample-row.mjs:77-81`). UNVERIFIED: that Claude Code 2.1.311 needs no endpoint, beta, top-level field, tool shape, content-block type, source type or model outside the allowlists (`api-proxy.mjs:12-13`; `eval/metrics/README.md:82-84`, `eval/metrics/README.md:151-165`, `eval/metrics/README.md:205-208`). A denial line with no `cause` is how a gap shows, and it marks the sample incomplete (`ReleaseArmSampleRow.stop_signals`). A `cause:"inflight"` 429 does not; Claude Code is expected to retry it (`sample-row.mjs:121-123`; UNVERIFIED that it does). A `cause:"budget"` 429 does, with the stop signal `unit budget exhausted` (`sample-row.mjs:118-120`).

**Echo response body (test hook, not logged):** under `PROXY_UPSTREAM=echo` the HTTP response to the sample is `{"echo":true,"method":…,"path":…,"forwarded_header_names":[sorted names],"x_api_key_sha256":"<sha256 hex of the injected key>","usage":{"input_tokens":7,"output_tokens":11}}` (`api-proxy.mjs:262-270`). The request still goes through admission, the reservation and the `start` / `end` lines, and a `messages` request is charged 18 units. Tested: the sha equals the canary key's sha, and `x-forwarded-host` and `authorization` are not forwarded (`run-release-arms-docker-test.sh:108-109`).

---

### ReleaseArmSampleRow

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **Kind** | `persistence` |
| **Ingestion route** | JSONL at `<out>/samples.jsonl` (`run-release-arms.sh:370`). `<out>` is `--out DIR` or the default `$HOME/agentic-os-eval-runs/<YYYYMMDDTHHMMSSZ UTC>-release-arms`, made absolute, and refused when it has `.` / `..` segments or lies inside the repo (`run-release-arms.sh:69-70`, `run-release-arms.sh:181-190`; tested `run-release-arms-test.sh:364-365`). Only `samples.jsonl` is truncated, after the cost gate (`run-release-arms.sh:309-366`, `run-release-arms.sh:368-371`); other files of an existing `--out` are left in place. **Cost gate (changed),** docker only (`run-release-arms.sh:327-355`): it prints the per-sample hard cap in cap units, the `max_tokens` ceiling, and that at most `PROXY_MAX_INFLIGHT` requests are in flight with the excess getting an immediate 429 (`run-release-arms.sh:333`); the comment above it states the admission rule (`run-release-arms.sh:328-330`). The per-sample bound itself still holds under the same assumption, because admission counts every in-flight reservation (`api-proxy.mjs:229`). Then `worst case: <samples> sample(s) x <PROXY_MAX_UNITS + PROXY_MAX_TOKENS + PROXY_RESERVE_OVERHEAD> units = <product> units` (`run-release-arms.sh:331-334`). A **dollar ceiling** is printed only when `PRICE_<KEY>_IN_PER_MTOK` / `_OUT_PER_MTOK` for both `--model` and `--small-model` plus `PRICE_CACHE_READ_MULT` and `PRICE_CACHE_WRITE_MULT` are all non-empty. It is units x the largest per-unit price over both models of `max(in, in x write mult, out, in x read mult / read weight)` / 1e6 (`run-release-arms.sh:335-351`). Otherwise it prints `dollar ceiling: price unknown (set <missing names>); cost will be reported as tokens only.` (`run-release-arms.sh:353`; tested `run-release-arms-docker-test.sh:120-121`). The gate checks only that a price is non-empty; the row builder also requires a finite number `>= 0` (`sample-row.mjs:29`). The "worst case" holds only when each response's units stay within its reservation (see `ReleaseArmProxyLogLine` Reservation; the README says it "holds while upstream usage ≤ the reservation", `eval/metrics/README.md:292-294`). One row per sample, appended after that sample's prompts and scoring, in run order (`run-release-arms.sh:617-700`). **Start failures (changed):** a docker sample whose start failed still gets a row with no prompts run and `SAMPLE_ERROR="could not start the sample container"` (`run-release-arms.sh:628-636`). After **two consecutive** start failures the run writes that row, stops, still writes `summary.json`, and exits 1 (`run-release-arms.sh:635`, `run-release-arms.sh:699`, `run-release-arms.sh:702`, `run-release-arms.sh:711`). Test hook `AOS_TEST_FAIL_START` (any non-empty value) makes every start fail before any docker object is created (`run-release-arms.sh:468-469`; tested `run-release-arms-docker-test.sh:129-135`). **Interrupt:** on SIGINT / SIGTERM (trapped, `run-release-arms.sh:397-403`) the current sample still gets its row, no later sample starts, `summary.json` is still written, and the run exits 130 (`run-release-arms.sh:621`, `run-release-arms.sh:698`, `run-release-arms.sh:702`, `run-release-arms.sh:710`; tested `run-release-arms-test.sh:378-395`). Each row is built by the working-tree `node eval/metrics/lib/sample-row.mjs` from environment variables only. It is piped through `lib/redact.mjs` before `>>`, and a failed write aborts the run (`run-release-arms.sh:690-697`; `sample-row.mjs:2-4`). **Inputs set by the harness** (`run-release-arms.sh:690-696`): `RUN_ID`, `HARNESS_SHA`, `HARNESS_DIRTY`, `SANDBOX`, `ARM`, `REF`, `FLAGS`, `REPO_SHA`, `SCENARIO`, `PROMPTS`, `SAMPLE`, `ORDER`, `SID`, `EXIT_CODES`, `INTERRUPTED`, `SAMPLE_ERROR`, `MODEL`, **new in round 7: `SMALL_MODEL`, `SET_MAX_TOKENS`, `SET_MAX_UNITS`, `SET_MAX_INFLIGHT`, `SET_CACHE_READ_WEIGHT`, `SET_BETAS_SHA256`** (`run-release-arms.sh:694-695`; see `settings`), `SCORE_JSON`, `LOG`, `PROXY_LOG`, `TASK_BASE`. File inputs (`sample-row.mjs:12-14`): (1) `SCORE_JSON` = `<scratch>/scores/<k>.json`, unredacted (`run-release-arms.sh:625`). Under `docker` it is the scorer's raw stdout (`run-release-arms.sh:571`, `run-release-arms.sh:672`); under `none` the stdout with `transcript` removed (`run-release-arms.sh:675-683`). A missing, empty or unparseable file is read as `{}` (`sample-row.mjs:24-27`, `sample-row.mjs:35`). (2) `LOG` = `<out>/claude-<tag>.log`, truncated at the sample's start, then a `--- prompt N ---` line and the redacted stdout+stderr of each `claude -p` (`run-release-arms.sh:624`, `run-release-arms.sh:647-653`). (3) `PROXY_LOG` = `<out>/proxy-<tag>.log`, passed under both modes (`run-release-arms.sh:696`). Under `none` the file normally does not exist, so `proxy` is `null` (`sample-row.mjs:70`), but a stale `proxy-<tag>.log` left by an earlier docker run into the same `--out` would be read. **Prices (changed):** none are built in, and the harness does not set them. The builder reads them from the environment it inherits from the operator: `PRICE_<KEY>_IN_PER_MTOK` / `PRICE_<KEY>_OUT_PER_MTOK` (USD per 1e6 tokens; `<KEY>` = the model id upper-cased with every non-`[A-Z0-9]` character replaced by `_`, e.g. `PRICE_CLAUDE_SONNET_5_5_IN_PER_MTOK`), `PRICE_CACHE_READ_MULT` and `PRICE_CACHE_WRITE_MULT` (multipliers of the input price), all UNVERIFIED (`sample-row.mjs:15-20`, `sample-row.mjs:132-136`; `run-release-arms.sh:98-101`). The round 4 inputs `PRICE_IN_PER_MTOK` / `PRICE_OUT_PER_MTOK` and their `3` / `15` defaults are **removed**. Still removed (since round 3): `METRICS_JSON`, `EXPLOIT_JSON`, `SCAN_JSON`, `TRANSCRIPT`. Read by `summarize-samples.mjs` (`ReleaseArmSummary`). Read by `lib/smoke-check.mjs`, from the **first** row of `samples.jsonl`: `cli_results`, `tokens.{input,cache_read,cache_creation,output,cap_units}`, `cost_usd`, `cost_source` (`smoke-check.mjs:20-21`, `smoke-check.mjs:32`, `smoke-check.mjs:57`). And by `run-release-arms-test.sh` (`run-release-arms-test.sh:236-265`, `run-release-arms-test.sh:325-356`, `run-release-arms-test.sh:393-394`, smoke `run-release-arms-test.sh:397-408`) and `run-release-arms-docker-test.sh` (`run-release-arms-docker-test.sh:91-98`, `run-release-arms-docker-test.sh:118-119`, `run-release-arms-docker-test.sh:134-135`). Join to `ArmIsolationRecord` on `session_id` (and `run_id`). Join to the `ReleaseArmScore` / `ReleaseArmProxyLogLine` files by `<tag>` = `<arm>-<scenario>-r<sample>` (`run-release-arms.sh:623`). |
| **Source** | `eval/metrics/lib/sample-row.mjs` (lines 1-250; field order 204-250); `eval/metrics/run-release-arms.sh` (lines 69-70, 93-115, 140-196, 232-307, 309-371, 397-459, 461-587, 617-711); `eval/metrics/lib/api-proxy.mjs` (`ReleaseArmProxyLogLine`); `eval/metrics/lib/score-sample.mjs` (`ReleaseArmScore`); `eval/metrics/lib/smoke-check.mjs` (reader only); `eval/metrics/lib/scan-task.sh`; `eval/metrics/lib/redact.mjs`; `eval/metrics/lib/scenario-prompts.mjs` (prompt count); `eval/metrics/scenarios/seeded-path-traversal/exploit-check.mjs` (`exploit_detail`); `eval/metrics/docker/Dockerfile`; `eval/metrics/docker/Dockerfile.scorer`; prose `eval/metrics/README.md` ("Release arms", lines 53-377; "Operator procedure", lines 86-102; "UNVERIFIED (all in one place)", lines 353-376) |

**Shape** (one line per sample; field order as written by `sample-row.mjs:204-250`; values illustrative, no prices supplied):

```json
{"run_id":"<uuid>","recorded_utc":"2026-10-28T12:00:00.000Z","harness_sha":"e601224","harness_dirty":false,"sandbox":"docker","arm":"v301","ref":"v3.0.1","flags":[],"repo_sha":"<40 hex>","model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","settings":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","small_model":"claude-haiku-5-5","max_tokens_cap":32000,"unit_cap":2000000,"max_inflight":8,"cache_read_weight":0.1,"betas_sha256":"<64 hex>"},"scenario":"seeded-path-traversal","prompts":1,"prompts_run":1,"status":"complete","incomplete_reason":null,"stop_signals":[],"advisory_stop_signals":[],"sample":1,"order":0,"session_id":"<uuid>","exit_code":0,"exit_codes":[0],"cli_results":1,"tokens":{"input":12,"cache_read":50000,"cache_creation":1000,"output":300,"by_model":{"claude-sonnet-5-5":{"input":12,"cache_read":50000,"cache_creation":1000,"output":300}},"cap_units":6312,"source":"proxy"},"cost_usd":null,"cost_source":"price unknown","price_missing":["PRICE_CLAUDE_SONNET_5_5_IN_PER_MTOK","PRICE_CLAUDE_SONNET_5_5_OUT_PER_MTOK","PRICE_CACHE_READ_MULT","PRICE_CACHE_WRITE_MULT"],"cli_cost_usd":0.0091,"cost_mismatch_usd":null,"proxy":{"summary":true,"requests":1,"committed_units":6312,"unparsed":0,"unfinished":0,"by_model":{"claude-sonnet-5-5":{"input":12,"cache_read":50000,"cache_creation":1000,"output":300}},"client_aborted":0,"inflight_rejected":0,"budget_rejected":0,"drain_rejected":0,"max_inflight_seen":1,"denials":0},"transcript_found":true,"metrics":{"transcript":"/tmp/score/transcript.jsonl","turns":12,"skipped_lines":0,"tokens":{"output":0,"input":0,"cache_read":0,"cache_creation":0},"output_tokens_per_turn":0,"awareness_signals":{"repeat_read_files":0,"repeat_reads":[],"repeated_tool_calls":0}},"metrics_trust":"model-writable (advisory)","compact_boundaries":0,"task_base":"<40 hex>","exploit":"fail","exploit_detail":{"exit":1,"leaked":["raw-dotdot"],"vectors":11},"scan":{"exit":0,"gitleaks":0,"semgrep":0,"dependency-audit":"unavailable"}}
```

**Trust model** (`sample-row.mjs:6-10`; `eval/metrics/README.md:220-231`). The proxy log is authoritative for `tokens`, `proxy`, denials and worst-case charges. `cost_usd` is a derived estimate from proxy tokens and operator-supplied prices. The CLI log (`cli_results`, `cli_cost_usd`, `advisory_stop_signals`) and the transcript (`metrics`, `compact_boundaries`, `transcript_error_results`) are model-writable and advisory. Stops are read from structured fields only (tested: free text with stop words stays `complete`, `run-release-arms-test.sh:348-349`).

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `run_id` | string (UUID) | yes | `crypto.randomUUID()` once per run (`run-release-arms.sh:192-193`). Same value as the run's `ArmIsolationRecord` lines and the `aos-run` docker label (`run-release-arms.sh:443`, `run-release-arms.sh:690`; `sample-row.mjs:205`) |
| `recorded_utc` | string (ISO 8601 UTC) | yes | `new Date().toISOString()` when the row is built, after the sample ran and was scored (`sample-row.mjs:206`) |
| `harness_sha` | string | yes | `git rev-parse --short HEAD` of the harness repo (abbreviated), or `"unknown"` (`run-release-arms.sh:195`; `sample-row.mjs:207`). The committed HEAD that the tasks, scenarios and all scoring code were exported from: `session-metrics.mjs`, `lib/score-sample.mjs`, `lib/scan-task.sh`, `lib/api-proxy.mjs` (`run-release-arms.sh:274-277`), `scripts/security-scan.sh` and the security skill scripts (`run-release-arms.sh:278-279`), each scenario's `exploit-check.mjs` and pristine `app/` (`run-release-arms.sh:291-295`), and `docker/Dockerfile` + `docker/Dockerfile.scorer` (`run-release-arms.sh:377-378`) |
| `harness_dirty` | boolean | yes | `iso_harness_dirty` once per run (`run-release-arms.sh:196`; `arm-isolation.sh:292-296`), written as `=== 'true'` (`sample-row.mjs:208`). `true` means `eval/metrics/` had uncommitted changes. These run from the working tree (`$DIR`): `run-release-arms.sh` itself, `lib/arm-isolation.sh` (`run-release-arms.sh:88`), `lib/redact.mjs` (`run-release-arms.sh:200`), `lib/scenario-prompts.mjs` (`run-release-arms.sh:301`), **`lib/smoke-check.mjs` (new, `run-release-arms.sh:614`)**, `lib/sample-row.mjs` (`run-release-arms.sh:697`) and `summarize-samples.mjs` (`run-release-arms.sh:702`, `run-release-arms.sh:704`). All scoring code and the proxy are HEAD copies (see `harness_sha`) |
| `sandbox` | enum string | yes | `docker` \| `none`: the `--sandbox` value, default `docker`; anything else is refused (`run-release-arms.sh:114`, `run-release-arms.sh:127`, `run-release-arms.sh:147`; passed `run-release-arms.sh:690`; `sample-row.mjs:209`). `docker`: a per-sample model container, proxy container and scorer container (`run-release-arms.sh:18-32`, `run-release-arms.sh:461-587`). `none`: model and scorer run on the host with **no containment**, for stub-claude tests only (`run-release-arms.sh:33-34`, `run-release-arms.sh:357`). Tested: `docker` on both rows of the docker test (`run-release-arms-docker-test.sh:92-93`) |
| `arm` | string | yes | Arm name from `NAME=REF[:FLAGS]`, `^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_-]*$`, unique per run (`run-release-arms.sh:239-240`; `sample-row.mjs:210`) |
| `ref` | string | yes | The REF exactly as the operator wrote it; non-empty and not starting with `-` (`run-release-arms.sh:241`; `sample-row.mjs:211`) |
| `flags` | array of string | yes | The arm's install flags, split on `,` with empty entries dropped (`sample-row.mjs:212`). Each matches `^--with-[a-z0-9-]+$` and names a pack the ref has (`run-release-arms.sh:244-257`). `[]` when the arm spec has no `:FLAGS` |
| `repo_sha` | string | yes | **Full 40-hex** commit sha of `ref`, from `git rev-parse --verify --quiet "<ref>^{commit}"` (`run-release-arms.sh:242`; `sample-row.mjs:213`). Unlike `run-arms.sh`'s `--short` `repo_sha` (`run-arms.sh:51`) |
| `model` | string \| null | yes | **New.** `MODEL` = the `--model` value, default `claude-sonnet-5-5` (`run-release-arms.sh:102`, `run-release-arms.sh:115`, `run-release-arms.sh:129`, `run-release-arms.sh:693`; `sample-row.mjs:214`); `null` only for a direct caller without `MODEL`. The eval model only. The background model (`--small-model`, default `claude-haiku-5-5`, `run-release-arms.sh:103`, `run-release-arms.sh:130`) is not recorded here; its tokens appear under its id in `tokens.by_model`. `--model` is passed to every `claude -p` (`CLAUDE_OPTS`, `run-release-arms.sh:457`; tested `run-release-arms-test.sh:403-404`). Under docker the prompt env also sets `ANTHROPIC_MODEL` = `ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_SONNET_MODEL` = `CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_MODEL` = `--model` and `ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_HAIKU_MODEL` = `--small-model` (`run-release-arms.sh:543-544`). The two models are the proxy's whole model allowlist (`run-release-arms.sh:486`). UNVERIFIED: the model ids, and that this pinning covers every call Claude Code makes (`run-release-arms.sh:94`; `eval/metrics/README.md:75-84`) |
| `settings` | object | yes | **New (round 7).** The run's settings, the same on every row of a run, so the summary can refuse to compare runs that differ (`sample-row.mjs:215-221`). `{model, small_model, max_tokens_cap, unit_cap, max_inflight, cache_read_weight, betas_sha256}`. `model` = `MODEL` or `null` (the same value as the row's `model`); `small_model` = `SMALL_MODEL` or `null`. The four numbers are `Number()` of `SET_MAX_TOKENS`, `SET_MAX_UNITS`, `SET_MAX_INFLIGHT` and `SET_CACHE_READ_WEIGHT` when finite, else `null`. An unset variable gives `null`, but an empty one gives `0` (`Number('')` is `0`). `betas_sha256` = `SET_BETAS_SHA256` or `null`. The harness passes `--small-model`, `PROXY_MAX_TOKENS`, `PROXY_MAX_UNITS`, `PROXY_MAX_INFLIGHT`, `PROXY_CACHE_READ_WEIGHT` and `BETAS_SHA256` under both `--sandbox` modes (`run-release-arms.sh:694-695`). `BETAS_SHA256` is the sha256 hex of `PROXY_BETAS` split on whitespace, one per line, `LC_ALL=C sort -u` (so order and duplicates do not matter) (`run-release-arms.sh:197-198`; `iso_sha256`, `arm-isolation.sh:58-61`). These are the harness's values, not the proxy's effective ones: the harness does not validate `PROXY_MAX_INFLIGHT`, so a `0` or non-numeric value is recorded as given (`0` or `null`) while the proxy uses `8` (`api-proxy.mjs:80`). Under `none` no proxy runs, but the values are still recorded. Read by `summarize-samples.mjs` as one JSON string per row (`ReleaseArmSummary.settings`). Tested: `claude-sonnet-5-5\|claude-haiku-5-5\|32000\|2000000\|8\|0.1\|string` on a `--sandbox none` run (`run-release-arms-test.sh:430-431`) |
| `scenario` | string | yes | Scenario name, `^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_-]*$` (`run-release-arms.sh:284`; `sample-row.mjs:222`). Resolved in the HEAD export as `eval/metrics/scenarios/<name>.md` (repo-local task) or `eval/metrics/scenarios/<name>/README.md` + `app/` (app task) (`run-release-arms.sh:285-299`) |
| `prompts` | integer | yes | Number of prompts the sample is scheduled to run: the bare ```` ``` ```` fences under `## Scripted prompt sequence` (`scenario-prompts.mjs:5-8`, `scenario-prompts.mjs:21-32`; split at `run-release-arms.sh:301-303`; `sample-row.mjs:34`, `sample-row.mjs:223`). `--smoke` keeps only the first 2 (`run-release-arms.sh:158-163`, `run-release-arms.sh:302`). At most one `claude -p` per prompt |
| `prompts_run` | integer | yes | `exit_codes.length` (`sample-row.mjs:32`, `sample-row.mjs:224`). Less than `prompts` when a prompt exited non-zero (the loop breaks there), when an interrupt arrived before a later prompt, when the installed config changed before a later prompt, or `0` when the docker sample failed to start (`run-release-arms.sh:640-667`). Tested: `3` for a sample stopped at prompt 3 of 10 (`run-release-arms-test.sh:337-338`) |
| `status` | enum string | yes | `complete` \| `incomplete`. It is `incomplete` exactly when `incomplete_reason` is non-null (`sample-row.mjs:225`). `complete` = every prompt ran and exited 0, `stop_signals` is empty (so a proxy summary was written if there was a proxy log, and there was no proxy denial, no client abort, no drain rejection, no worst-case charge, no unit-budget 429, no interrupt, no sample error and no advisory signal; a `cause:"inflight"` 429 alone does not prevent it), and `metrics` is non-null. `summarize-samples.mjs` does not read this field; it recomputes completeness (`summarize-samples.mjs:43-47`). Tested: the docker test's adversarial stub sends denied probes, so both docker rows are `incomplete` with `stopped: proxy denied: …` (`run-release-arms-docker-test.sh:92-93`) |
| `incomplete_reason` | string \| null | yes | `null` when complete. Otherwise the first match of (`sample-row.mjs:170-176`, `sample-row.mjs:226`): (1) `interrupted (K of M prompts ran)`, when `INTERRUPTED=1` (`sample-row.mjs:171`). (2) **New:** the `SAMPLE_ERROR` text, cut to 120 characters (`sample-row.mjs:172`). The harness sets `could not start the sample container` (`run-release-arms.sh:632`) or `installed config changed between prompts` (`run-release-arms.sh:644`). (3) `prompt N of M exited C`: N = 1-based position of the first non-zero entry of `exit_codes`, M = `prompts`, C = that code (`sample-row.mjs:173`). (4) `K of M prompts ran` (`sample-row.mjs:174`). (5) `stopped: <stop_signals[0]>` (`sample-row.mjs:175`). (6) `no transcript metrics`, when `metrics` is null (`sample-row.mjs:176`). **Interrupt detection (changed):** the sample is marked interrupted when the trap already fired, checked before each prompt (`run-release-arms.sh:642`). It is also marked when, after a prompt, the trap had fired or the harness's own pipeline member `redact` exited with status `>= 128` (killed by a signal). The CLI's own exit code is not used for this (`run-release-arms.sh:655-660`). Either way the run then stops (`run-release-arms.sh:698`). Tested: the stub sends SIGINT to the run's whole process group mid-prompt, and the sample is `incomplete` with `interrupted` in `stop_signals` (`run-release-arms-test.sh:65-67`, `run-release-arms-test.sh:378-395`). UNVERIFIED how the real `claude` / `docker exec` behave on a terminal Ctrl-C (`smoke-check.mjs:74` leaves it as a manual item). Tested: `prompt 3 of 10 exited 1` (`run-release-arms-test.sh:337-338`), `stopped: cli result is_error (error_max_turns)` with exit 0 (`run-release-arms-test.sh:350-351`), `could not start the sample container` (`run-release-arms-docker-test.sh:134-135`) |
| `stop_signals` | array of string | yes | **Changed.** Distinct entries (`new Set`, `sample-row.mjs:169`, `sample-row.mjs:227`), in this order. (a) **New:** `no proxy summary` when the proxy log has no `summary:true` line (`sample-row.mjs:107`). (b) `proxy denied: <status> <denied, cut to 100>` for the **first** denial line whose `cause` is none of `drain`, `inflight`, `budget`, whenever the log has any such line (`sample-row.mjs:83-86`, `sample-row.mjs:110`). Any other `cause`, such as a round-7 log's `cap`, is now read as a denial. (c) **New:** `client aborted request` when `proxy.client_aborted` is non-zero (`sample-row.mjs:111-113`). (d) `proxy rejected N request(s) while shutting down (not model-caused)` when `proxy.drain_rejected` is non-zero (`sample-row.mjs:114-116`). (e) `proxy charged worst case (unparsed U, unfinished F)` when either count is non-zero (`sample-row.mjs:117`). A charged client abort also counts in `unparsed`, so it gives (c) and (e). (f) **New in round 8:** `unit budget exhausted` when `proxy.budget_rejected` is non-zero: later work was refused, so the sample is cut short even if the CLI exited 0 (`sample-row.mjs:118-120`). (g) `interrupted` (`sample-row.mjs:167`). (h) the `SAMPLE_ERROR` text, cut to 120 (`sample-row.mjs:168`). (i) Every entry of `advisory_stop_signals`, folded in, so a model can drop its own sample but cannot forge `complete` (`sample-row.mjs:169`). **Not a stop signal:** a `cause:"inflight"` 429 (in-flight limit); it is counted in `proxy.inflight_rejected` only, on the assumption that the CLI retries it (`sample-row.mjs:84`, `sample-row.mjs:121-123`; the comment marks that UNVERIFIED). An upstream 429 is not a denial line either. `[]` when none. Exit codes are not listed; they drive `incomplete_reason` directly. Tested: a start with no end and no summary gives `stopped: no proxy summary` (`run-release-arms-test.sh:414-416`); a drain 503 with a summary gives `stopped: proxy rejected 1 request(s) while shutting down (not model-caused)` (`run-release-arms-test.sh:417-419`); an aborted request gives `incomplete`, `stopped: client aborted request` (`run-release-arms-test.sh:420-423`); an inflight 429 gives `denials 0`, `inflight_rejected 1` and no stop signal (`run-release-arms-test.sh:424-426`); a budget 429 with exit 0 gives `incomplete`, `stopped: unit budget exhausted`, `denials 0` (`run-release-arms-test.sh:427-429`) |
| `advisory_stop_signals` | array of string | yes | Distinct entries (`sample-row.mjs:228`; position moved to after `stop_signals`). (a) From the **last** CLI result record in `LOG` only (`sample-row.mjs:54-59`): `cli result is_error (<subtype or "error", cut to 60>)` when `is_error === true`, else `cli result subtype <subtype, cut to 60>` when `subtype` is truthy and not `success`. (b) Then one `transcript error result (<s>)` per entry of the score's `transcript_error_results` (`sample-row.mjs:60-62`). Tested: `cli result is_error (error_max_turns)` (`run-release-arms-test.sh:350-351`). UNVERIFIED: the result-record and transcript error-record shapes (`score-sample.mjs:79`) |
| `sample` | integer | yes | 1-based round number `r` (`run-release-arms.sh:415`, `run-release-arms.sh:692`; `sample-row.mjs:229`) |
| `order` | integer | yes | 0-based position in the run sequence (`k`, `run-release-arms.sh:620`, `run-release-arms.sh:692`; `sample-row.mjs:230`). Samples are ordered round, then scenario, then arms rotated by one per round (`run-release-arms.sh:415-419`). Tested `l.every((r,i)=>r.order===i)` (`run-release-arms-test.sh:245`) |
| `session_id` | string (UUID) | yes | Per-sample `crypto.randomUUID()` (`run-release-arms.sh:440`). Prompt 1 gets `--session-id <id>`, later prompts `--resume <id>` (`run-release-arms.sh:646`). The scorer finds the transcript by it (`score-sample.mjs:39-51`; `sample-row.mjs:231`) |
| `exit_code` | integer | yes | First non-zero entry of `exit_codes`, else `0` (`sample-row.mjs:33`, `sample-row.mjs:232`) |
| `exit_codes` | array of integer | yes | Exit status of each prompt that **ran**, in order: `PIPESTATUS[0]` of the prompt pipeline (`run-release-arms.sh:650`, `run-release-arms.sh:653`, `run-release-arms.sh:655`, `run-release-arms.sh:661`; `sample-row.mjs:32`, `sample-row.mjs:233`). Under `docker` it is the status of `docker exec … claude` (`docker_prompt`, `run-release-arms.sh:535-546`, `run-release-arms.sh:649`), so a docker-side failure also shows as non-zero. Under `none` it is the status of `iso_exec … claude` (`run-release-arms.sh:652`). The harness stops a sample at the first non-zero exit and does not `--resume` past it (`run-release-arms.sh:662-665`), so a non-zero entry can only be the last one. `[]` when the docker sample failed to start. UNVERIFIED whether hitting `--max-turns` or `--max-budget-usd` exits non-zero (`eval/metrics/README.md:287`) |
| `cli_results` | integer | yes | **New.** Number of CLI result records found in `LOG` (`sample-row.mjs:40-47`, `sample-row.mjs:234`). A result record is a trimmed log line that starts with `{`, ends with `}`, parses, and has `type === "result"`. A record printed across several lines is not seen. Smoke item 2 passes only when this is `> 0` (`smoke-check.mjs:32`; tested `run-release-arms-test.sh:400`, `run-release-arms-test.sh:405-408`) |
| `tokens` | object \| null | yes | **Changed.** `{input, cache_read, cache_creation, output, by_model, cap_units, source: "proxy"}` whenever the proxy log was read (`sample-row.mjs:128-131`, `sample-row.mjs:235`). The four counts are the sums over `proxy.by_model`. `input` is now the API's uncached `input_tokens` only, and the cache fields are separate (see `ReleaseArmProxyLogLine.usage`). `by_model` = `proxy.by_model`; `cap_units` = `proxy.committed_units`. These are **charged** tokens: with a summary line they include the worst-case bookings for unparsed and unfinished requests. In the no-summary rebuild those bookings are in `cap_units` but not in the four counts (see `proxy`). `null` when there is no proxy log, i.e. `--sandbox none` (`sample-row.mjs:127`). Never taken from the CLI log or the transcript. Tested: docker rows carry `input > 0`, a `cache_read` key and `cap_units > 0` (`run-release-arms-docker-test.sh:118-119`); none rows have `null` (`run-release-arms-test.sh:355-356`) |
| `cost_usd` | number \| null | yes | **Changed** (`sample-row.mjs:126-165`, `sample-row.mjs:236`). With a proxy log: (a) **new:** no proxy summary line: `null`, whatever prices are supplied (`sample-row.mjs:146-150`). (b) A summary with an empty `by_model` and `committed_units > 0`: `null` (`sample-row.mjs:151-154`). A real proxy summary cannot produce this, since every charge is also booked to `by_model` (`charge`, `api-proxy.mjs:212-218`); only a hand-made or corrupted summary can. (c) Empty `by_model` and `committed_units` `0`: `0` (`sample-row.mjs:155`). (d) Any price missing (see `price_missing`): `null` (`sample-row.mjs:157`). (e) Otherwise the sum over models of `(input x IN + output x OUT + cache_read x IN x CACHE_READ_MULT + cache_creation x IN x CACHE_WRITE_MULT) / 1e6`, rounded to 6 decimals (`sample-row.mjs:141-143`, `sample-row.mjs:159`). Without a proxy log: (f) `cli_cost_usd`, unrounded, when non-null (`sample-row.mjs:163-164`); (g) else `null`. There is no CLI fallback when a proxy log exists. A price counts only when it is set, non-empty, a finite number and `>= 0` (`envNum`, `sample-row.mjs:29`). Tested: `null` with no prices on docker rows (`run-release-arms-docker-test.sh:118-119`); `5` for 10 stub results of `0.5` under none (`run-release-arms-test.sh:355-356`); `null` for a log with one unmatched `start` reserving 5000 and no summary (`run-release-arms-test.sh:414-416`) |
| `cost_source` | enum string \| null | yes | Matching `cost_usd` (a) to (g) (`sample-row.mjs:146-164`, `sample-row.mjs:237`): (a) **new** `unknown: no proxy summary (<committed_units> cap units, <reserved_unfinished_units> reserved for unfinished requests)` (`sample-row.mjs:149`); (b) `unknown: <committed_units> cap units charged with no usage attributed` (`sample-row.mjs:154`); (c) `proxy: no billable requests` (`sample-row.mjs:155`); (d) `price unknown` (`sample-row.mjs:157`); (e) `proxy tokens x supplied prices (estimate)`, or `proxy tokens x supplied prices (estimate; includes worst-case charges)` when `proxy.unparsed` or `proxy.unfinished` is non-zero (`sample-row.mjs:160`); (f) `cli-only (model-writable)` (`sample-row.mjs:164`); (g) `null`. Values (a) and (b) embed numbers, so the set of strings is open. **Changed:** a docker row whose proxy log has no summary (killed, crashed, never started) now always gets (a); `price_missing` is still computed for it. A failed start whose proxy never ran leaves only docker's error text, so it gets `unknown: no proxy summary (0 cap units, 0 reserved for unfinished requests)` (round 6: `proxy: no billable requests`); one whose proxy ran and was stopped gets (c). Counted per group by `summarize-samples.mjs` (`ReleaseArmSummary.cost_sources`). Tested: `unknown: no proxy summary (5000 cap units, 5000 reserved for unfinished requests)` (`run-release-arms-test.sh:414-416`) |
| `price_missing` | array of string | yes | **New.** Distinct names of the price variables that are needed but not usable, in first-seen order (`sample-row.mjs:134-140`, `sample-row.mjs:145`, `sample-row.mjs:238`): `PRICE_<KEY>_IN_PER_MTOK` / `PRICE_<KEY>_OUT_PER_MTOK` for each model in `proxy.by_model`; `PRICE_CACHE_READ_MULT` only when some model has `cache_read > 0`; `PRICE_CACHE_WRITE_MULT` only when some model has `cache_creation > 0`. `[]` without a proxy log or with an empty `by_model` |
| `cli_cost_usd` | number \| null | yes | Advisory. Sum of `total_cost_usd` over the CLI result records, counting only finite numbers `>= 0`, not rounded (`sample-row.mjs:49-50`, `sample-row.mjs:239`). `null` when there is no such value. Tested: a forged negative cost is ignored (`run-release-arms-test.sh:352-353`). Every invocation gets `--output-format json` (`run-release-arms.sh:457`), and stderr is merged into the log (`run-release-arms.sh:649`, `run-release-arms.sh:652`). UNVERIFIED: that Claude Code 2.1.311 prints its result record as one line with `type`, `subtype`, `is_error` and `total_cost_usd`, and whether `total_cost_usd` is per call or a running total on `--resume` (`smoke-check.mjs:55`; `eval/metrics/README.md:288-291`) |
| `cost_mismatch_usd` | number \| null | yes | `cli_cost_usd - cost_usd`, rounded to 6 decimals, only for `cost_source` (e) (either `proxy tokens x supplied prices …` value) with a non-null `cli_cost_usd`; else `null` (`sample-row.mjs:158-162`, `sample-row.mjs:240`). Positive = the CLI's estimate is higher. A flag only; it does not affect `status` |
| `proxy` | object \| null | yes | **Changed.** `null` when `PROXY_LOG` is unset or the file is missing (`sample-row.mjs:70`). Lines are read as latin1, trimmed, and kept only if they start with `{` and parse to an object (`sample-row.mjs:77-81`). Each kept line is classified (`sample-row.mjs:82-94`): a `summary: true` line (the last one wins); a `denied` line with `cause:"drain"` (drain count), **`cause:"inflight"` or `cause:"budget"` (inflight / budget counts, new in round 8, replacing `cap`)**, or with any other or no `cause` (a denial); a `start` line (counted, kept open); an `end` line (closes its `id`; `committed` = the largest `end.committed`; **changed:** counted as unparsed when `usage_parsed === false` and `units > 0`, no longer by a 2xx status; **new:** counted as aborted when `note` is `client aborted request`; non-null `usage` summed per `model`). **With a summary line** (`sample-row.mjs:96-101`): `{summary: true, requests, committed_units, unparsed, unfinished, by_model, client_aborted, inflight_rejected, budget_rejected, drain_rejected, max_inflight_seen}`. `requests`, `committed_units`, `unparsed`, `unfinished` and `client_aborted` come from the summary, each `0` when not a finite number; `by_model` = its `by_model` or `{}`, not validated. `inflight_rejected`, `budget_rejected` and `drain_rejected` = the summary's when finite, else the line counts. A round-7 summary's `cap_rejected` is not read. **New** `max_inflight_seen` = the summary's when finite, else `null`. **Without one** (`summary: false`: proxy killed, crashed or never started; `sample-row.mjs:102-107`): `{summary: false, requests, committed_units, reserved_unfinished_units, unparsed, unfinished, by_model, client_aborted, inflight_rejected, budget_rejected, drain_rejected, max_inflight_seen: null}`, all from the lines. `requests` = the `start` lines; `unfinished` = the starts with no end; **new** `reserved_unfinished_units` = the sum of their `reserved` (present only in this case); `committed_units` = the largest `end.committed` + `reserved_unfinished_units` (fail closed); `client_aborted` is now rebuilt from the `end` notes. The row also gets the stop signal `no proxy summary` and cost (a) (see `cost_source`). In both cases (`sample-row.mjs:109-110`): `denials` = the number of denial lines with no `drain`, `inflight` or `budget` cause, counted from the lines, not taken from the summary; `denied` = the first 5 of those as `<status> <denied, cut to 100>`, present only when there are any. **Removed in round 8:** `cap_rejected`. **Removed in round 7:** `dropped` and `queue_wait_ms`. If the proxy never ran, the file holds only docker's error text, which gives `{summary: false, requests: 0, committed_units: 0, reserved_unfinished_units: 0, unparsed: 0, unfinished: 0, by_model: {}, client_aborted: 0, inflight_rejected: 0, budget_rejected: 0, drain_rejected: 0, max_inflight_seen: null, denials: 0}`, zero `tokens` and `cost_usd` `null`. Tested: `proxy.denials >= 6` on the docker rows (`run-release-arms-docker-test.sh:92-93`); `denials 0`, `drain_rejected 1` (`run-release-arms-test.sh:417-419`); `tokens.cap_units 900` for one aborted request (`run-release-arms-test.sh:420-423`); `denials 0`, `inflight_rejected 1` (`run-release-arms-test.sh:424-426`); `denials 0` for a budget 429 (`run-release-arms-test.sh:427-429`) |
| `transcript_found` | boolean | yes | `score.transcript_found === true` (`sample-row.mjs:242`); see `ReleaseArmScore`. Also `false` when the score file is missing, empty or malformed |
| `metrics` | object \| null | yes | Advisory (model-writable). The score's `metrics` when it is an object, else `null` (`sample-row.mjs:36`, `sample-row.mjs:243`). It is `computeMetrics` of `session-metrics.mjs` over the scorer's capped copy of the transcript (`score-sample.mjs:72-74`): `{transcript, turns, skipped_lines, tokens: {output, input, cache_read, cache_creation}, output_tokens_per_turn, awareness_signals: {repeat_read_files, repeat_reads, repeated_tool_calls}}` (`session-metrics.mjs:87-98`). `transcript` is the scorer's work path, not the published file: `/tmp/score/transcript.jsonl` in the docker scorer (`run-release-arms.sh:571`), `<scratch>/scores/<k>-work/transcript.jsonl` under `none` (`run-release-arms.sh:676`). The published copy is `<out>/transcripts/<tag>.jsonl` (docker `run-release-arms.sh:548-558`; none `run-release-arms.sh:684`). Also measured for a stopped sample (which is then `incomplete`). `null` when there is no transcript or the scorer produced no parsable output |
| `metrics_trust` | string | yes | Always the constant `"model-writable (advisory)"` (`sample-row.mjs:244`). A label, not a computed value |
| `compact_boundaries` | integer \| null | yes | Advisory. The score's `compact_boundaries` when it is a number, else `null` (`sample-row.mjs:245`): the count of transcript lines with `subtype === "compact_boundary"` (`score-sample.mjs:75-80`). UNVERIFIED: that record shape against a live headless transcript (`score-sample.mjs:79`; `eval/metrics/README.md:356-360`). `0` may mean no compaction, or a different shape. Tested: `9` for a 10-prompt stub run (`run-release-arms-test.sh:325-326`) |
| `task_base` | string (40 hex) \| null | yes | App scenarios: `git rev-parse HEAD` of the task dir after one `task base` commit of the copied `app/`, made on the host before the model runs (`run-release-arms.sh:432-439`; passed `run-release-arms.sh:696`; `sample-row.mjs:246`). Not the scanner's `--base`: `scan-task.sh` builds its own repo and base commit, and that sha is not written (`scan-task.sh:31-36`, `scan-task.sh:52`). `null` for repo-local scenarios |
| `exploit` | enum string \| null | yes | From the score's `exploit` (`sample-row.mjs:178-189`, `sample-row.mjs:247`): `json.exploit` when it is `pass` / `fail` / `error`, otherwise `error`. `null` when the score has no `exploit`: the staged harness has no `scenarios/<name>/exploit-check.mjs` (`score-sample.mjs:86-87`; staged only for app scenarios that have one, `run-release-arms.sh:291-295`), or the score was empty. The check is a HEAD copy that the scorer runs on a copy of the task (see `ReleaseArmScore.exploit`). Under `docker` it exists only in the scorer image, not in the sample's image (`docker/Dockerfile:40-43`; `docker/Dockerfile.scorer:1-6`), and the app runs as uid 2000 (`run-release-arms.sh:571`; `exploit-check.mjs:92-108`, `exploit-check.mjs:158`). For seeded-path-traversal: `pass` = the feature works and no vector leaked a canary; `fail` = at least one vector leaked; `error` = task rejected, app did not load / start, feature missing, or timeout (`exploit-check.mjs:8-13`, `exploit-check.mjs:59-90`, `exploit-check.mjs:110-191`). The check's own timeout is `EXPLOIT_CHECK_TIMEOUT_MS` or 60000 ms (`exploit-check.mjs:194-200`). The scorer gives the check only `PATH`, `HOME`, `TMPDIR` and `EXPLOIT_APP_UID` (`score-sample.mjs:98-99`), so 60 s applies, under a 180 s spawn timeout (`score-sample.mjs:100`). Tested: the docker test's vulnerable app still scores `fail` despite the stub's forgery attempts (`run-release-arms-docker-test.sh:96-97`, `run-release-arms-docker-test.sh:99`) |
| `exploit_detail` | object \| null | yes | `null` when `exploit` is `null`. Otherwise `{exit, ...rest}`, where `exit` = `Number(score.exploit.rc)` and `rest` = the check's JSON minus `exploit` (`sample-row.mjs:182-184`), or `{exit, reason: "exploit check produced no verdict"}` (`sample-row.mjs:185-188`). seeded-path-traversal `rest` on `pass` / `fail`: `leaked` (vector labels, `[]` on pass) and `vectors` (count tried, 11) (`exploit-check.mjs:133-145`, `exploit-check.mjs:181-186`). On `error`, `reason`, one of: `task dir is a symlink` (`exploit-check.mjs:115`), `task contains a symlink: <rel>` (`exploit-check.mjs:68`), `task contains a non-regular file: <rel>` (`exploit-check.mjs:71`), `not a dir inside the work dir: <rel>` (`exploit-check.mjs:82`), `write target escapes the work dir: <rel>` (`exploit-check.mjs:85`), `write target is not a regular file: <rel>` (`exploit-check.mjs:88`), `server did not start: <first stderr line, or timeout>` (`exploit-check.mjs:175`), `feature missing: GET /files/hello.txt -> <status>` (`exploit-check.mjs:179`), `timeout` (`exploit-check.mjs:198`), any other thrown message (`exploit-check.mjs:187-188`), or, from the scorer, `could not copy the task dir` with exit 2 (`score-sample.mjs:93-94`). `.git` and `node_modules` entries are skipped, not rejected (`exploit-check.mjs:65`); the scorer's copy already has every `.git` excluded (`score-sample.mjs:92`). Check exit codes: 0 pass / 1 fail / 2 error (`exploit-check.mjs:37`, `exploit-check.mjs:199`). The scorer records 2 when the check was killed (`score-sample.mjs:103`). Tested: planted symlinks give `error`, exit 2, a `symlink` reason (`run-release-arms-test.sh:155-156`) |
| `scan` | object \| null | yes | `null` when the score has no `scan` (no exploit check; `sample-row.mjs:191-192`). Otherwise `exit` = `Number(score.scan.rc)` (`sample-row.mjs:194`). That is the exit of `security-scan.sh` (codes per `ReviewScanReport`), which `scan-task.sh` `exec`s (`scan-task.sh:54-57`). It is `2` when the scan repo could not be built or its base sha not read (`scan-task.sh:48-53`), when the task copy failed (`score-sample.mjs:95`), or when the scan was killed or passed its 900 s timeout (`score-sample.mjs:105-109`). `scan-task.sh <task copy> <pristine app> <work> <scanner>` (`score-sample.mjs:105`) builds a fresh `<work>/scan-repo` (`scan-task.sh:31-47`): the pristine HEAD app as the `task base` commit, then the task's files minus every `.git` (links copied as links) as the working tree. It then removes every `.gitignore` and `.gitattributes` file, every nested `.git`, empties `.git/info/exclude`, and runs `git add -f -A`, so the model cannot hide a planted file from the scan (`scan-task.sh:39-46`). Every git call runs with `env -i`, its own `HOME`, a scrubbed `SCAN_PATH`, `GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1`, `GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null`, `core.hooksPath=/dev/null` and an empty `core.fsmonitor` (`scan-task.sh:26-29`). `SCAN_PATH` = `/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin` plus the dirs of `git jq node semgrep gitleaks osv-scanner` found on the scorer's `PATH`, the only env var the scorer passes in (`scan-task.sh:18-24`; `score-sample.mjs:106`). The scan runs `security-scan.sh --base <that base> --json` under `env -i` with `PATH`, `HOME`, `TMPDIR`, the two `GIT_CONFIG_*`, `XDG_CONFIG_HOME`, `XDG_CACHE_HOME` and `SECURITY_SCAN_OFFLINE=1` (`scan-task.sh:54-57`). The task dir's own `.git` is never used (tested `run-release-arms-test.sh:261-262`). Then, when `score.scan.json` has a `scanners` array: `gitleaks` and `semgrep` default to `"unavailable"`, then there is one key per reported scanner `name` (`ReviewScanReport` names). Its value is the finding count for status `ran` / `findings`, else the status string (`unavailable` \| `error`); on a repeated name the last entry wins (`sample-row.mjs:195-198`). Without a report: `{exit, error: "security-scan.sh produced no JSON report"}` (`sample-row.mjs:199-201`). The image pins semgrep 1.182.1 and gitleaks 8.30.1 (`docker/Dockerfile:9-35`; the scorer image inherits them, `docker/Dockerfile.scorer:7-8`), so docker rows normally carry integer counts (tested `run-release-arms-docker-test.sh:98`). Offline mode makes `dependency-audit` `unavailable` when a lockfile exists (see `ReviewScanReport`) |

Redaction. Every file `run-release-arms.sh` writes to `<out>` goes through `lib/redact.mjs` (`redact`, `run-release-arms.sh:200`; uses `run-release-arms.sh:556`, `run-release-arms.sh:581`, `run-release-arms.sh:649`, `run-release-arms.sh:652`, `run-release-arms.sh:684`, `run-release-arms.sh:686-687`, `run-release-arms.sh:697`), with three exceptions. `isolation.jsonl` and `summary.json` are not redacted. `install-<tag>.log` is written directly by `iso_install_library`, whose `env -i` holds no auth variable (`run-release-arms.sh:430`; `arm-isolation.sh:122-126`). `redact.mjs` replaces the values (8 characters or longer) of `REDACT_VARS`, default `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN`, with `[REDACTED:<NAME>]`. Output past `REDACT_CAP_BYTES` (default 50 MiB) is dropped after a `\n[truncated at <cap> bytes]\n` marker (`redact.mjs:6-7`, `redact.mjs:11-15`, `redact.mjs:23`, `redact.mjs:29-34`). The harness sets neither variable. Tested: the key canary never reaches `<out>`, and the log shows `key=[REDACTED:ANTHROPIC_API_KEY]` (`run-release-arms-test.sh:366-367`; docker `run-release-arms-docker-test.sh:122`). Smoke item 5 also checks that neither the raw key nor its sha256 appears in any file under `<out>` (`smoke-check.mjs:59-71`; tested `run-release-arms-test.sh:402`). Docker test allowlist of `<out>` files: `isolation.jsonl`, `samples.jsonl`, `summary.json`, `{claude,proxy,install}-<tag>.log`, `score-<tag>.{json,err}`, `transcripts/<tag>.jsonl`, and no symlinks (`run-release-arms-docker-test.sh:123-124`; `eval/metrics/README.md:338-354`). The smoke checklist and probe result are only printed (`run-release-arms.sh:606`, `run-release-arms.sh:706`; `smoke-check.mjs:15`).

---

### ReleaseArmScore

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **Kind** | `persistence` |
| **Ingestion route** | One JSON line on stdout of `node lib/score-sample.mjs --root <sample root> --sid <session id> --scenario <name> --harness <dir> --work <empty dir> [--app-uid N] [--emit-transcript]` (`score-sample.mjs:12-15`, `score-sample.mjs:21-24`, `score-sample.mjs:113`). Missing args, a `sid` not matching `^[0-9a-f-]{36}$` or a bad scenario name exit 2 with no stdout (`score-sample.mjs:25-29`). `score-sample.mjs` is unchanged in rounds 4 and 5. The code is the HEAD copy staged into the harness dir, never the working tree (`run-release-arms.sh:270-279`). **`--sandbox docker`:** the harness is baked only into the **scorer** image at `/opt/harness` (`docker/Dockerfile.scorer:10-12`, `FROM` the base image, built at `run-release-arms.sh:387-390`). The base image, which the sample runs from, holds only the proxy (`docker/Dockerfile:40-45`). `docker_score` removes the sample container, then runs a fresh scorer container (`run-release-arms.sh:560-572`): `--network none`, `--user 0:0` with only `SETUID` / `SETGID` / `KILL` / `DAC_READ_SEARCH`, `no-new-privileges`, read-only root fs, tmpfs `/tmp`, pids limit 512, and the sample volume read-only at `/sample`. Args: `--root /sample --sid <sid> --scenario <name> --harness /opt/harness --work /tmp/score --app-uid 2000`, with **no `--emit-transcript`**. Its stdout goes straight to `<scratch>/scores/<k>.json` and its stderr to `<scratch>/scores/<k>.err`; the exit status is ignored (`run-release-arms.sh:625`, `run-release-arms.sh:672`). The host does not parse or normalize that stdout: a malformed or empty one is published as is, and `sample-row.mjs` reads it as `{}`. Order per sample: proxy capture, transcript copy, scoring, then `docker_end_sample` removes the containers **and the volume** (`run-release-arms.sh:583-587`, `run-release-arms.sh:669-673`). So the volume does not survive a scorer crash; the comment at `run-release-arms.sh:561-562` holds only for `docker_score` itself. **Transcript under docker:** it is copied out separately, before scoring, by `docker_save_transcript` (`run-release-arms.sh:548-558`, called `run-release-arms.sh:671`). That is a throwaway base-image container with `--network none`, uid 1000, `--cap-drop ALL`, a read-only root fs and the volume read-only. It takes the first hit of `find /sample/home/.claude/projects -name <sid>.jsonl -type f`, reads at most 52428800 bytes (50 MiB) with `head -c`, and writes it through `redact.mjs` to `<out>/transcripts/<tag>.jsonl`; the file is deleted when empty. This is a separate lookup from the scorer's own (`score-sample.mjs:39-51`, sorted directory order and per-component symlink checks). If several project dirs held `<sid>.jsonl`, the two could pick different files. **`--sandbox none`:** run on the host with `--harness <scratch harness> --work <scratch>/scores/<k>-work --emit-transcript`, without `--app-uid` (`run-release-arms.sh:675-676`). Stdout goes to `<scratch>/scores/<k>-full.json`, stderr to `<scratch>/scores/<k>.err`. The host then parses it (malformed or empty -> `{}`), writes a string `transcript` to `<scratch>/scores/<k>.transcript`, deletes the key, and writes the rest, without a trailing newline, to `<scratch>/scores/<k>.json`. If that node step fails, it writes `{}` (`run-release-arms.sh:677-683`). The transcript is published redacted as `<out>/transcripts/<tag>.jsonl` (`run-release-arms.sh:684`). **Both modes:** `<out>/score-<tag>.json` = `<k>.json` through `redact.mjs` (`run-release-arms.sh:686`), plus `<out>/score-<tag>.err` when `<k>.err` exists (`run-release-arms.sh:687`). After a failed docker start, `<k>.json` stays empty (`run-release-arms.sh:625`) and no `.err` exists, so `score-<tag>.json` is an empty file. `<tag>` = `<arm>-<scenario>-r<round>` (`run-release-arms.sh:623`). Consumers: `sample-row.mjs` reads the **unredacted scratch** `<k>.json` as `SCORE_JSON` (`run-release-arms.sh:696`; `ReleaseArmSampleRow`); the per-sample progress line reads `.exploit.json.exploit` and `.scan.rc` with `jq` (`run-release-arms.sh:688`). The scratch dirs are removed at exit (`run-release-arms.sh:218`, `run-release-arms.sh:227-230`, `run-release-arms.sh:618`). |
| **Source** | `eval/metrics/lib/score-sample.mjs` (lines 1-113); `eval/metrics/lib/scan-task.sh` (lines 1-57); `eval/metrics/run-release-arms.sh` (lines 270-279, 373-390, 548-587, 617-697); `eval/metrics/docker/Dockerfile` (lines 40-45); `eval/metrics/docker/Dockerfile.scorer` (lines 1-13); `eval/metrics/lib/redact.mjs`; prose `eval/metrics/README.md` ("Sandbox", lines 200-209; "What the harness does", lines 289-304) |

**Shape** (`<out>/score-<tag>.json`; key order of the initial object, `score-sample.mjs:32-35`; `transcript` is appended last only under `--sandbox none` on the raw stdout, and is never in the published file; values illustrative):

```json
{"transcript_found":true,"transcript_truncated":false,"metrics":{"transcript":"/tmp/score/transcript.jsonl","turns":12,"skipped_lines":0,"tokens":{"output":0,"input":0,"cache_read":0,"cache_creation":0},"output_tokens_per_turn":0,"awareness_signals":{"repeat_read_files":0,"repeat_reads":[],"repeated_tool_calls":0}},"compact_boundaries":0,"transcript_error_results":[],"exploit":{"rc":1,"json":{"exploit":"fail","leaked":["raw-dotdot"],"vectors":11}},"scan":{"rc":0,"json":{"scanners":[]},"stderr":""}}
```

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `transcript_found` | boolean | yes | `true` when `<root>/home/.claude/projects/<dir>/<sid>.jsonl` exists for some `<dir>` (sorted, first match). `home`, `.claude`, `projects` and `<dir>` must be real directories and the file a regular file, none a symlink, and the file is read through `O_NOFOLLOW` (`score-sample.mjs:37-51`, `score-sample.mjs:55-69`) |
| `transcript_truncated` | boolean | yes | `true` when the transcript is larger than 50 MiB; only the first 50 MiB are copied to `<work>/transcript.jsonl` and scored (`score-sample.mjs:31`, `score-sample.mjs:58-68`). `false` when there is no transcript. Not copied into `ReleaseArmSampleRow` |
| `metrics` | object \| null | yes | `computeMetrics(<work>/transcript.jsonl)` from the staged `session-metrics.mjs` (`score-sample.mjs:72-74`; shape `session-metrics.mjs:87-98`, see `ReleaseArmSampleRow.metrics`). `null` without a transcript |
| `compact_boundaries` | integer \| null | yes | Lines of the copied transcript whose JSON has `subtype === "compact_boundary"`; unparseable and empty lines are skipped (`score-sample.mjs:75-80`). `null` without a transcript. UNVERIFIED record shape (`score-sample.mjs:79`) |
| `transcript_error_results` | array of string | yes | For each transcript line with `type === "result"` and `is_error === true`: its `subtype`, or `"error"`, cut to 80 characters (`score-sample.mjs:81`). `[]` without a transcript. Each entry becomes `transcript error result (<s>)` in the row's `advisory_stop_signals`, and so also in `stop_signals` (`sample-row.mjs:60-62`, `sample-row.mjs:169`). UNVERIFIED record shape (`score-sample.mjs:79`) |
| `exploit` | object \| null | yes | `null` when `<harness>/scenarios/<scenario>/exploit-check.mjs` does not exist (`score-sample.mjs:86-87`). Otherwise the task dir is copied with `tar --exclude=.git` and `--no-same-owner` into `<work>/task`, links kept as links (`score-sample.mjs:88-92`). The check then runs as `node <check> <work>/task --json` with env `PATH` (the scorer's) and `HOME=TMPDIR=<work>/xtmp`, plus `EXPLOIT_APP_UID=<--app-uid>` when given, under a 180 s timeout and a 1 MiB output buffer (`score-sample.mjs:97-100`). `{rc, json}`: `rc` = the check's exit status, or `2` when it was killed (`status` null, e.g. timeout) (`score-sample.mjs:103`); `json` = the last stdout line parsed as JSON, or `null` (`score-sample.mjs:101-102`). The check's stderr is discarded. When the task copy fails: `{rc: 2, json: {exploit: "error", reason: "could not copy the task dir"}}` (`score-sample.mjs:93-94`) |
| `scan` | object \| null | yes | `null` when `exploit` is `null`. Otherwise `bash <harness>/lib/scan-task.sh <work>/task <harness>/scenarios/<scenario>/app <work>/scan <harness>/scanner`, with env `PATH` only, a 900 s timeout and a 64 MiB buffer (`score-sample.mjs:105-106`; for the repo build and scan see `ReleaseArmSampleRow.scan`). `{rc, json, stderr}`: `rc` = exit status, or `2` when killed (`score-sample.mjs:109`); `json` = the whole stdout parsed (a `ReviewScanReport`), or `null` (`score-sample.mjs:107-108`); `stderr` = the last 2000 characters of `scan-task.sh`'s stderr, which includes the repo-build output (`scan-task.sh:48-49`; `score-sample.mjs:109`). When the task copy fails: `{rc: 2, json: null}`, with no `stderr` key (`score-sample.mjs:95`) |
| `transcript` | string | no | Only with `--emit-transcript` and a found transcript: the capped copy decoded as latin1 (`score-sample.mjs:83`). The harness passes the flag only under `--sandbox none` (`run-release-arms.sh:676`). It then removes the key before writing `<k>.json`, and publishes the text as `<out>/transcripts/<tag>.jsonl`, written back as latin1 so the bytes round-trip, then redacted (`run-release-arms.sh:680-684`). Under `docker` the key is never present (`run-release-arms.sh:570-571`); the transcript comes from `docker_save_transcript` instead (see Ingestion route) |

If the scorer crashes or exits 2 on its arguments, its stdout is empty and the stored score is empty (docker) or `{}` (none) (`run-release-arms.sh:672`, `run-release-arms.sh:677-683`). Both read as `{}` (`sample-row.mjs:24-27`, `sample-row.mjs:35`). `ReleaseArmSampleRow` then has `transcript_found: false`, and `metrics`, `compact_boundaries`, `exploit`, `exploit_detail` and `scan` all `null`. The sample is `incomplete` with `no transcript metrics`, unless an earlier reason applies (`sample-row.mjs:170-176`). `score-<tag>.err` holds the cause. Under docker the transcript was already saved by then (`run-release-arms.sh:671`).

---

### ReleaseArmSummary

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **Kind** | `persistence` |
| **Ingestion route** | `node eval/metrics/summarize-samples.mjs <samples.jsonl> --json` stdout (working tree, `$DIR`), written to `<out>/summary.json` at the end of a `run-release-arms.sh` run, under both `--sandbox` modes, after an interrupt, and after the two-start-failure abort (`run-release-arms.sh:702`; tested `run-release-arms-test.sh:392`, `run-release-arms-docker-test.sh:134-135`). A failure aborts with `summary failed`. Not passed through `redact.mjs`, but every string field is stripped of control characters (see Group). The same script is then run without `--json` to print the text summary (`run-release-arms.sh:704`). The JSON is one pretty-printed object (`JSON.stringify(s, null, 2)`, `summarize-samples.mjs:229-230`), not JSONL. The text form is not part of this contract (`summarize-samples.mjs:231-260`). Its group header prints `samples=`, `complete=`, `incomplete=`, `completion=<P>%`, `proxy_denials=<n>` and, **new in round 8**, `inflight_429s=<proxy_inflight_429s> budget_429s=<proxy_budget_429s>` (replacing `proxy_cap_429s=`) (`summarize-samples.mjs:234`), then **(new, round 6)** a `  cost: <n> x <cost_source>; …[; missing <names>]` line (`summarize-samples.mjs:235`; tested `run-release-arms-test.sh:472`), and it keeps a `WARNING: only <c> of <n> samples complete for <arm>; …` line per group with incomplete samples (`summarize-samples.mjs:236-238`; tested `run-release-arms-test.sh:354`). **Changed (round 8):** the text ends with one `ARMS COMPARABLE? yes\|NO (one run and sandbox, same model/harness/settings, same prompt counts; <comparable.rule>; runs=<runs>, required=<required_complete>)` line, with no reasons on it, then one `  NO because: <reason>` line per entry of `comparable.reasons`, then **(new)** one `  WARNING: <warning>` line per entry of `comparable.warnings` (`summarize-samples.mjs:246-249`; tested `run-release-arms-test.sh:448-458`; the `WARNING:` line is tested at `run-release-arms-test.sh:459-462`). **New in round 9:** then a deltas block (`summarize-samples.mjs:250-259`). When `deltas` is null it prints one line, `DELTAS: not comparable: <comparable.reasons, cleaned, joined by "; ">`. Otherwise it prints a `DELTAS (arm - baseline; mean, [range of per-sample differences], n arm/baseline):` header. Then, per entry, a `  <scenario>: <arm> - <baseline>` line, and one line per row: `    primary ` or `    advisory`, then the metric padded to 28, `delta_mean` padded to 12, `  [<lo>, <hi>]  n=<arm n>/<baseline n>`. It ends with a caveat line: `  primary = proxy-authoritative; advisory = model-writable transcript.`, followed on the same line by ` compact_boundaries: the record shape and whether headless --resume compacts are UNVERIFIED.` only when some Delta has a `compact_boundaries` row (**changed in round 10**, `summarize-samples.mjs:258`) Tested: three `v301` rows with `turns` 1 and three `main` rows with `turns` 4 print `  s: main - v301`, an `advisory turns … 3  [3, 3]  n=3/3` line and the caveat without the `compact_boundaries` clause; `v301` rows with `compact_boundaries` 1, 0, 0 and `main` rows with 1, 1, 0 print `advisory compact_boundaries … 0.33  [-1, 1]  n=3/3` and the clause; the two-failure fixture prints `DELTAS: not comparable: ` (`run-release-arms-test.sh:463-471`). The README's operator procedure tells the operator to read `ARMS COMPARABLE?` and the `DELTAS` block (`eval/metrics/README.md:105-109`), which also states the baseline rule (`eval/metrics/README.md:107-109`). As the README says (`eval/metrics/README.md:319-320`), the header shows the completion rate and the inflight and budget 429s per arm; `proxy_inflight_429s_per_sample` is in the JSON only. Also importable as `summarize(rows)` (`summarize-samples.mjs:60`), plus the exported constants `MIN_COMPLETE = 2` (`summarize-samples.mjs:189`) and, **new in round 9**, `BASELINE_ARM = 'v301'` (`summarize-samples.mjs:152`). **Removed in round 8:** the exports `MIN_RATE` and `RATE_TOLERANCE`. Input: every non-empty line of a `ReleaseArmSampleRow` JSONL, `JSON.parse`d; a bad line throws (`summarize-samples.mjs:227`). No path argument exits 2 (`summarize-samples.mjs:226`). Deterministic (Tier 0): independent of row order and timestamps (`summarize-samples.mjs:5-7`; tested `run-release-arms-test.sh:305-307`). Descriptive only, not a significance test (`summarize-samples.mjs:9-10`). |
| **Source** | `eval/metrics/summarize-samples.mjs` (lines 1-261); `eval/metrics/run-release-arms.sh` (lines 702-704); `eval/metrics/run-release-arms-test.sh` (lines 437-468); prose `eval/metrics/README.md` ("What the harness does", "Summary", lines 305-327; "Operator procedure" step 4, lines 99-101; the `--runs 3` comment, lines 68-69) |

**Shape** (`summarize-samples.mjs:117-144`):

```json
{
  "groups": [
    {
      "scenario": "seeded-path-traversal",
      "arm": "main",
      "refs": ["main@<40 hex>"],
      "samples": 3,
      "complete": 1,
      "incomplete": {"count": 2, "reasons": {"could not start the sample container": 1, "stopped: proxy denied: 400 tool type not allowed: web_search_20250305": 1}},
      "proxy_denials": 1,
      "proxy_inflight_429s": 0,
      "proxy_inflight_429s_per_sample": 0,
      "proxy_budget_429s": 0,
      "completion_rate": 0.333,
      "run_ids": ["<uuid>"],
      "sandboxes": ["docker"],
      "settings": ["{\"model\":\"claude-sonnet-5-5\",\"small_model\":\"claude-haiku-5-5\",\"max_tokens_cap\":32000,\"unit_cap\":2000000,\"max_inflight\":8,\"cache_read_weight\":0.1,\"betas_sha256\":\"<64 hex>\"}"],
      "cost_sources": {"none": 1, "price unknown": 2},
      "price_missing": ["PRICE_CACHE_READ_MULT", "PRICE_CLAUDE_SONNET_5_5_IN_PER_MTOK"],
      "models": ["claude-sonnet-5-5"],
      "prompts": [1],
      "harness": ["e601224"],
      "primary": {
        "proxy_input_tokens": {"n": 1, "mean": 12, "min": 12, "max": 12},
        "proxy_cache_read_tokens": {"n": 1, "mean": 50000, "min": 50000, "max": 50000},
        "proxy_cache_creation_tokens": {"n": 1, "mean": 1000, "min": 1000, "max": 1000},
        "proxy_output_tokens": {"n": 1, "mean": 300, "min": 300, "max": 300},
        "proxy_cap_units": {"n": 1, "mean": 6312, "min": 6312, "max": 6312},
        "cost_usd_estimate": {"n": 0, "mean": null, "min": null, "max": null},
        "cli_cost_usd": {"n": 1, "mean": 0.01, "min": 0.0091, "max": 0.0091}
      },
      "metrics": {
        "turns": {"n": 1, "mean": 14, "min": 14, "max": 14},
        "output_tokens": {"n": 0, "mean": null, "min": null, "max": null}
      },
      "metrics_trust": "awareness metrics are model-writable (advisory); primary tokens/cost are proxy-authoritative",
      "exploit": {"pass": 1, "fail": 0, "error": 0, "not_applicable": 0}
    }
  ],
  "comparable": {"ok": false, "rule": "each (scenario, arm) >= max(2, runs-1) complete; complete counts per scenario differ by <= 1", "runs": 3, "required_complete": 2, "reasons": ["seeded-path-traversal/main: 1 of 3 complete < 2 required (rule: max(2, runs-1) with runs=3)"], "warnings": []},
  "deltas": null
}
```

`deltas` when `comparable.ok` is `true` (illustrative; `summarize-samples.mjs:153-172`):

```json
"deltas": [
  {"scenario": "seeded-path-traversal", "arm": "main", "baseline": "v301", "rows": [
    {"kind": "primary", "metric": "proxy_cap_units", "delta_mean": 812.5, "range": [-400, 2100], "n": [3, 3]},
    {"kind": "advisory", "metric": "turns", "delta_mean": 3, "range": [3, 3], "n": [3, 3]}
  ]}
]
```

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `groups` | array of Group | yes | One per distinct raw `(scenario, arm)` pair, sorted by raw `scenario` then raw `arm` in code-point order (`summarize-samples.mjs:61-70`) |
| `comparable` | object | yes | **Changed (round 8).** One verdict for the whole run, `{ok, rule, runs, required_complete, reasons, warnings}` (`summarize-samples.mjs:143-144`, `summarize-samples.mjs:190-222`). The round-7 `min_complete`, `min_rate` and `rate_tolerance` keys are gone, and so are the rate checks. `rule` = the constant string `each (scenario, arm) >= max(2, runs-1) complete; complete counts per scenario differ by <= 1`. `runs` = the largest `samples` of any group (`0` with no groups), which is `--runs` when every sample got a row. `required_complete` = `max(MIN_COMPLETE, runs - 1)` with `MIN_COMPLETE` = `2` (`summarize-samples.mjs:189`, `summarize-samples.mjs:198-199`, `summarize-samples.mjs:221`). The code comment calls this count rule a maintainer decision: one failure per arm per scenario is tolerated, and 2 is the fewest samples that give any spread (`summarize-samples.mjs:180-185`). `reasons` is an array of strings, in this order. (1) For each of `run_ids`, `sandboxes`, `models`, `harness`, `settings`, in that order: `<label> differ (<count>)` when the union of that group field over all groups does not have exactly one value; labels `run ids`, `sandbox modes`, `models`, `harness shas`, `per-run settings` (`summarize-samples.mjs:193-197`). (2) Per scenario, sorted: `<scenario>: prompt counts differ across arms` when the union of its groups' `prompts` is not exactly one value; `<scenario>: arm <arm> has no samples` for every arm seen anywhere in the run but not in that scenario; and, **new**, `<scenario>: complete counts differ by <hi-lo> (> 1): <arm>=<complete>, …` (arms sorted) when the largest minus the smallest `complete` of its groups is over 1 (`summarize-samples.mjs:208-213`). (3) In group order, per group, **changed:** `<scenario>/<arm>: <complete> of <samples> complete < <required_complete> required (rule: max(2, runs-1) with runs=<runs>)` when `complete < required_complete`, with `; with --runs 1 this is always NO` appended when `runs < 2` (`summarize-samples.mjs:218-220`). `ok` = `reasons` is empty (`summarize-samples.mjs:221`). **New** `warnings` is an array of strings that never affects `ok`: per scenario, sorted, `<scenario>: inflight 429s per sample far apart (<arm>=<rate>, …); a fan-out arm may have been throttled (UNVERIFIED whether the CLI retries transparently)` when the largest `proxy_inflight_429s_per_sample` of its groups is over 10 and over 3 x the smallest (arms in group order) (`summarize-samples.mjs:214-216`). A scenario with one arm never warns. Scenario and arm names in the reasons and warnings are the cleaned group values. Not checked: `harness_dirty`, the CLI version, `completion_rate`, and whether `cost_source` agrees across arms. With no rows at all, every set is empty, so `ok` is `false` with the five `… differ (0)` reasons. Tested (`run-release-arms-test.sh:437-458`): `yes` for two arms with 2 of 2 complete from one run, sandbox and settings, and for 2 of 3 complete in both arms; `NO` with `s/a: 1 of 3 complete < 2 required (rule: max(2, runs-1) with runs=3)` and `s: complete counts differ by 2 (> 1): a=1, b=3`; `s/b: 1 of 1 complete < 2 required`; the `--runs 1` suffix; `run ids differ` and `sandbox modes differ`; `per-run settings differ`; and, for 10/10 vs 2/10 complete, `s/b: 2 of 10 complete < 9 required` and `s: complete counts differ by 8`. Tested `warnings`: per-sample inflight rates 200 vs 0 give `WARNING: s: inflight 429s per sample far apart (a=200, b=0)` and still `yes` (`run-release-arms-test.sh:459-462`) |
| `deltas` | array of Delta \| null | yes | **New in round 9.** `null` unless `comparable.ok` is `true` (`summarize-samples.mjs:143-144`). It is always present as a key. Otherwise it is computed by `deltas(groups)` (`summarize-samples.mjs:147-172`). It loops over scenarios, sorted by `cmp` (`summarize-samples.mjs:155`). Within each, the **baseline** is the group whose arm equals `BASELINE_ARM` = `v301`, else the first arm by name (`summarize-samples.mjs:152`, `summarize-samples.mjs:158`). The code comment calls `v301` "the release under comparison" (`summarize-samples.mjs:149-150`). There is one Delta per other arm of that scenario, sorted by arm (`summarize-samples.mjs:159`). A scenario with one arm gives no entry, so a comparable run with a single arm gives `[]`. **Delta** = `{scenario, arm, baseline, rows}` (`summarize-samples.mjs:168`). `scenario`, `arm` and `baseline` are the cleaned group values. Baseline selection and sorting use those cleaned values, unlike the grouping. `rows` is first the `primary` block's keys in group order, with `kind:"primary"`, then the `metrics` block's keys, with `kind:"advisory"` (`summarize-samples.mjs:161-162`). A metric is skipped when the baseline lacks it or either side's `mean` is `null` (`n: 0`) (`summarize-samples.mjs:163-164`). Row = `{kind, metric, delta_mean, range, n}` (`summarize-samples.mjs:165`). `delta_mean` = `round2(arm.rawMean - baseline.rawMean)`: **changed in round 10**, it uses the unrounded means, so 2/3 - 1/3 gives 0.33, not 0.67 - 0.33 = 0.34. `rawMean` is a non-enumerable property set by `stats()`, so it is not in `summary.json`; the Group `mean` stays rounded to 2 decimals (`summarize-samples.mjs:29-34`). `range` = `[round2(arm.min - baseline.max), round2(arm.max - baseline.min)]`: the code comment calls it the "range of possible per-sample differences" (`summarize-samples.mjs:147-149`). `n` = `[arm.n, baseline.n]`. Only complete rows feed these (see Group `primary` / `metrics`). Advisory rows are model-writable, and `compact_boundaries` carries the UNVERIFIED caveat printed in the text form. Descriptive only, not a significance test. Tested via the text output (`run-release-arms-test.sh:463-471`); no test reads the JSON `deltas` |

**Group** (field order `summarize-samples.mjs:117-141`). Round 5 added `proxy_denials`, renamed `primary.cost_usd` and added three `primary` keys. Round 6 added `cost_sources`, `price_missing`, `models`, `prompts` and `harness`. Round 7 added `proxy_cap_rejected`, `completion_rate`, `run_ids`, `sandboxes` and `settings`, and removed `primary.proxy_queue_wait_ms_total`. **Round 8** replaces `proxy_cap_rejected` with `proxy_inflight_429s`, `proxy_inflight_429s_per_sample` and `proxy_budget_429s`.

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `scenario` | string | yes | `clean(row.scenario)` (`summarize-samples.mjs:118`). `clean` removes C0 controls except tab, DEL and C1 (`/[\u0000-\u0008\u000a-\u001f\u007f-\u009f]/g`), then cuts to 300 characters (`summarize-samples.mjs:37-41`). Grouping and sorting use the raw values (`summarize-samples.mjs:63`, `summarize-samples.mjs:70`), so two raw names that clean to the same string give two groups with the same name. Tested: escapes stripped from scenario, arm and reasons in both JSON and text (`run-release-arms-test.sh:369-376`) |
| `arm` | string | yes | `clean(row.arm)` (`summarize-samples.mjs:119`) |
| `refs` | array of string | yes | Distinct raw `"<ref>@<repo_sha>"` of the group's rows (`summarize-samples.mjs:66`), each cleaned, then sorted (`summarize-samples.mjs:120`). Normally one entry |
| `samples` | integer | yes | Row count, complete and incomplete (`summarize-samples.mjs:121`) |
| `complete` | integer | yes | Rows that pass `isComplete` (`summarize-samples.mjs:43-47`, `summarize-samples.mjs:76`, `summarize-samples.mjs:122`): `exit_codes` is an array whose length equals `prompts`, every entry is `0`, `stop_signals` is empty (a missing or non-array value counts as empty, `summarize-samples.mjs:42`), and `metrics` is a truthy object. Recomputed from those raw fields; the row's `status` is not read (`summarize-samples.mjs:36`). Rows carry every proxy, interrupt, sample-error and advisory signal in `stop_signals` (`ReleaseArmSampleRow.stop_signals`), so any of them makes a row non-complete here too. Row `sandbox` and `advisory_stop_signals` are not read here |
| `incomplete` | object | yes | `{count, reasons}` (`summarize-samples.mjs:123`). `count` = `samples - complete`. `reasons` maps a reason string to its row count over the non-complete rows (`summarize-samples.mjs:77-82`); `{}` when every row is complete. **Changed:** `incompleteReason` returns the row's own `incomplete_reason` when it is a non-empty string, since rows are written by the host (`summarize-samples.mjs:48-51`). So the summary now also shows the `SAMPLE_ERROR` reasons. Only when that field is missing or empty does it recompute, in five forms (`summarize-samples.mjs:52-57`): `interrupted (K of M prompts ran)` when `stop_signals` holds `interrupted`, `prompt N of M exited C`, `K of M prompts ran`, `stopped: <first stop signal>`, `no transcript metrics` (a missing `exit_codes` is treated as `[]`). The recompute has no `SAMPLE_ERROR` form; for such a row it gives `K of M prompts ran` or `stopped: <signal>` instead. Keys are sorted by their raw string, then cleaned; counts of raw keys that clean to the same string are merged (`summarize-samples.mjs:83-86`). So the key order follows the raw sort and need not be sorted after cleaning. Tested: `{"count":2,"reasons":{"1 of 3 prompts ran":1,"prompt 1 of 1 exited 1":1}}` on hand-made rows without `incomplete_reason` (`run-release-arms-test.sh:177-187`). A stopped sample counts as incomplete (`run-release-arms-test.sh:339-340`) |
| `proxy_denials` | integer | yes | Sum of `Number(row.proxy.denials)` over **every** row of the group, complete or not; a missing or non-numeric value adds 0 (`summarize-samples.mjs:97-98`, `summarize-samples.mjs:124`). `0` for `--sandbox none` rows (`proxy: null`).
